"""
Briefings — conversa estruturada entre arquiteto e cliente.

============================================================================
REGRA ARQUITETURAL
============================================================================

`briefings` responde por:

    - modelos reutilizáveis de briefing (template)
    - execução de um briefing para um cliente específico
    - histórico imutável de eventos da conversa
    - projeção do estado atual de cada pergunta
    - transcrição e análise (IA) rastreáveis

`briefings` NÃO responde por:

    - identidade do usuário           → pertence a `accounts`
    - contexto do cliente             → pertence a `clients`
    - armazenamento de arquivos       → pertence a `documents`
    - projetos                        → pertence a `projects` (futuro)
    - timeline transversal            → pertence a `timeline` (futuro)

============================================================================
PRINCÍPIOS
============================================================================

1. Separação modelo / execução
   BriefingTemplate é reutilizável. Briefing é uma execução imutável
   para um cliente. Alterar o template NÃO altera execuções existentes
   (estrutura é copiada para um snapshot no momento da criação).

2. Tipo da pergunta ≠ formato da resposta
   `question_type` descreve a intenção do arquiteto.
   `allowed_response_formats` define o que o cliente pode enviar.

3. Histórico append-only
   BriefingEvent nunca é editado nem apagado. Cada resposta, dúvida,
   pedido de revisão ou intervenção é um novo evento.

4. Estado atual é projeção
   BriefingAnswer é a projeção do estado atual de uma pergunta.
   É derivada dos eventos, mas mantida explicitamente para consulta
   rápida. Respostas anteriores permanecem no histórico de eventos.

5. Transcrição e análise têm estado próprio
   Uma falha na transcrição NÃO invalida a resposta original.
   Uma análise antiga nunca parece atual (campo `is_stale`).

6. Rastreabilidade
   Cada item da análise aponta para a pergunta e o evento de origem.

============================================================================
MODELOS (V1)
============================================================================

BriefingTemplate        → modelo reutilizável criado pelo arquiteto
BriefingSection         → agrupamento de perguntas dentro do template
BriefingQuestion        → pergunta dentro de uma sessão do template

Briefing                → execução de um template para um cliente
BriefingAnswer          → projeção do estado atual de uma pergunta
BriefingEvent           → histórico imutável de eventos da conversa

============================================================================
FRONTEIRA CONGELADA (V1)
============================================================================

    accounts      → identidade
    workspaces    → tenancy
    console       → governança, capabilities, planos
    clients       → cliente e portal
    documents     → armazenamento de arquivos (imagens, áudio)
    briefings     → este app
    projects      → operação (futuro)
    timeline      → memória factual (futuro)
    intelligence  → interpretação (futuro)

"""

default_app_config = 'apps.briefings.apps.BriefingsConfig'

# =========================================================================
# INLINES

class BriefingQuestionInline(admin.TabularInline):
    model = BriefingQuestion
    extra = 0
    fields = [
        'text', 'question_type', 'allowed_response_formats',
        'position', 'is_required',
    ]
    ordering = ['position']
    show_change_link = True


class BriefingSectionInline(admin.TabularInline):
    model = BriefingSection
    extra = 0
    fields = ['title', 'description', 'position']
    ordering = ['position']
    show_change_link = True

@admin.register(BriefingTemplate)
class BriefingTemplateAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = [
        'name', 'workspace', 'category', 'status',
        'sections_count', 'questions_count',
        'created_at',
    ]
    list_filter = ['status', 'category', 'workspace']
    search_fields = ['name', 'description', 'category']
    readonly_fields = ['id', 'created_at', 'updated_at']
    ordering = ['-created_at']
    autocomplete_fields = ['workspace', 'created_by']
    inlines = [BriefingSectionInline]

    fieldsets = (
        ('Identificação', {
            'fields': ('id', 'workspace', 'name', 'description', 'category'),
        }),
        ('Editorial', {
            'fields': ('status',),
        }),
        ('Capa', {
            'fields': ('cover',),
        }),
        ('Rastreabilidade', {
            'fields': ('created_by', 'created_at', 'updated_at'),
            'classes': ('collapse',),
        }),
    )

@admin.register(BriefingEvent)
class BriefingEventAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = [
        'briefing', 'kind', 'author_display',
        'question_id_short', 'created_at',
    ]
    list_filter = ['kind']
    search_fields = ['briefing__title', 'content']
    readonly_fields = [
        'id', 'briefing', 'kind', 'question_id', 'content',
        'user', 'portal_session', 'created_at',
    ]
    autocomplete_fields = ['briefing', 'user', 'portal_session']
    ordering = ['-created_at']
    date_hierarchy = 'created_at'

    def author_display(self, obj):
        if obj.user_id:
            return obj.user.email
        if obj.portal_session_id:
            return f'portal:{obj.portal_session_id}'
        return '—'
    author_display.short_description = 'Autor'

    def question_id_short(self, obj):
        return str(obj.question_id)[:8] if obj.question_id else '—'
    question_id_short.short_description = 'Pergunta'

    def has_add_permission(self, request):
        return False

    def has_change_permission(self, request, obj=None):
        return False

    def has_delete_permission(self, request, obj=None):
        return False

"""
Serializers de Briefing (execução), BriefingAnswer e BriefingEvent.
"""

from rest_framework import serializers

from apps.briefings.models import (
    Briefing,
    BriefingAnswer,
    BriefingEvent,
)
from apps.briefings.services.briefing_service import BriefingService


# =========================================================================
# HELPERS
# =========================================================================

def _absolute_url(request, url):
    """
    Retorna URL absoluta quando possível.

    B2/R2 com `AWS_QUERYSTRING_AUTH=True` já devolvem URLs assinadas
    absolutas. Local devolve `/media/...` — precisa do request para
    virar absoluta.
    """
    if not url:
        return ''
    if url.startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
        return url
    if request is not None:
        try:
            return request.build_absolute_uri(url)
        except Exception:
            return url
    return url


def _briefing_cover_url(briefing, request=None):
    """
    Resolve a URL da capa de um briefing.

    A capa vive em `template.cover`. O briefing é uma execução — só
    referencia o template. Se o template foi excluído ou não tem
    capa, devolve string vazia.
    """
    template = briefing.template
    if template is None:
        return ''
    if not template.cover:
        return ''
    try:
        url = template.cover.url
    except Exception:
        return ''
    return _absolute_url(request, url)


# =========================================================================
# ANSWER (leitura)
# =========================================================================

class BriefingAnswerSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    state_display = serializers.CharField(
        source='get_state_display', read_only=True,
    )
    transcription_status_display = serializers.CharField(
        source='get_transcription_status_display', read_only=True,
    )
    latest_audio_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    attachment_ids = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = BriefingAnswer
        fields = [
            'id',
            'question_id',
            'state',
            'state_display',
            'latest_content',
            'latest_audio',
            'latest_audio_url',
            'latest_transcription',
            'transcription_status',
            'transcription_status_display',
            'latest_event',
            'attachment_ids',
            'created_at',
            'updated_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields

    def get_latest_audio_url(self, obj):
        if not obj.latest_audio or not obj.latest_audio.file:
            return ''
        try:
            return obj.latest_audio.file.url
        except Exception:
            return ''

    def get_attachment_ids(self, obj):
        return [str(d.id) for d in obj.attachments.all()]


# =========================================================================
# EVENT (leitura)
# =========================================================================

class BriefingEventSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    kind_display = serializers.CharField(
        source='get_kind_display', read_only=True,
    )
    author_email = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    author_kind = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = BriefingEvent
        fields = [
            'id',
            'kind',
            'kind_display',
            'question_id',
            'content',
            'author_email',
            'author_kind',
            'created_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields

    def get_author_email(self, obj):
        if obj.user:
            return obj.user.email
        if obj.portal_session and obj.portal_session.client:
            return obj.portal_session.client.email or ''
        return ''

    def get_author_kind(self, obj):
        if obj.user_id:
            return 'user'
        if obj.portal_session_id:
            return 'portal'
        return 'system'


# =========================================================================
# BRIEFING — listagem
# =========================================================================

class BriefingListSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    status_display = serializers.CharField(
        source='get_status_display', read_only=True,
    )
    client_name = serializers.CharField(
        source='client.name', read_only=True, allow_null=True,
    )
    template_name = serializers.CharField(
        source='template.name', read_only=True, allow_null=True,
    )
    cover_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    progress = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = Briefing
        fields = [
            'id',
            'title',
            'status',
            'status_display',
            'client',
            'client_name',
            'template',
            'template_name',
            'cover_url',
            'expires_at',
            'progress',
            'created_at',
            'updated_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields

    def get_cover_url(self, obj):
        request = self.context.get('request') if self.context else None
        return _briefing_cover_url(obj, request)

    def get_progress(self, obj):
        return BriefingService.compute_progress(obj)


# =========================================================================
# BRIEFING — detalhe
# =========================================================================

class BriefingDetailSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    status_display = serializers.CharField(
        source='get_status_display', read_only=True,
    )
    client_name = serializers.CharField(
        source='client.name', read_only=True, allow_null=True,
    )
    template_name = serializers.CharField(
        source='template.name', read_only=True, allow_null=True,
    )
    cover_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    progress = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    answers = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    workspace_id = serializers.UUIDField(
        source='workspace.id', read_only=True,
    )

    class Meta:
        model = Briefing
        fields = [
            'id',
            'workspace_id',
            'title',
            'status',
            'status_display',
            'client',
            'client_name',
            'template',
            'template_name',
            'cover_url',
            'structure_snapshot',
            'expires_at',
            'started_at',
            'completed_at',
            'cancelled_at',
            'progress',
            'answers',
            'created_at',
            'updated_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields

    def get_cover_url(self, obj):
        request = self.context.get('request') if self.context else None
        return _briefing_cover_url(obj, request)

    def get_progress(self, obj):
        return BriefingService.compute_progress(obj)

    def get_answers(self, obj):
        qs = obj.answers.select_related(
            'latest_event', 'latest_audio',
        ).prefetch_related('attachments').order_by('created_at')
        return BriefingAnswerSerializer(qs, many=True).data

        required=False, allow_blank=True, max_length=200,

        required=False, allow_null=True,
    )

        required=False, allow_blank=True, max_length=1000,
    )

"""
Serializers de BriefingNote (Journal do arquiteto).
"""

from rest_framework import serializers

from apps.briefings.models import BriefingNote


class BriefingNoteSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    author_email = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    author_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = BriefingNote
        fields = [
            'id',
            'briefing',
            'question_id',
            'author',
            'author_email',
            'author_name',
            'content',
            'created_at',
            'updated_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = [
            'id',
            'briefing',
            'author',
            'author_email',
            'author_name',
            'created_at',
            'updated_at',
        ]

    def get_author_email(self, obj):
        return obj.author.email if obj.author else None

    def get_author_name(self, obj):
        return obj.author.full_name if obj.author else None


class BriefingNoteCreateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    content = serializers.CharField(allow_blank=False, max_length=20000)
    question_id = serializers.UUIDField(
        required=False, allow_null=True,
    )

    def validate_content(self, value):
        value = (value or '').strip()
        if not value:
            raise serializers.ValidationError('A anotação não pode ser vazia.')
        return value


class BriefingNoteUpdateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    content = serializers.CharField(allow_blank=False, max_length=20000)

    def validate_content(self, value):
        value = (value or '').strip()
        if not value:
            raise serializers.ValidationError('A anotação não pode ser vazia.')
        return value

"""
Serializers do portal do cliente.

REGRA ARQUITETURAL
------------------
O serializer serve DADO BRUTO. Não resume, não cura, não analisa.
A análise pertence ao `intelligence` (futuro).

LINKS:
    portal_url                  → URL do chat do briefing (cliente).
    briefing_page_url           → URL da página do cliente (respostas + moodboard).
    briefing_pdf_url            → PDF do cliente (dados brutos).
    briefing_architect_url      → URL da página do arquiteto.
    briefing_architect_pdf_url  → PDF do arquiteto (dados brutos + transcrições).
"""

from rest_framework import serializers

from apps.briefings.models import Briefing, BriefingAnswer


def _doc_url(doc, request=None):
    if not doc or not doc.file:
        return ''
    try:
        url = doc.file.url
    except Exception:
        return ''
    if not url:
        return ''
    if request is not None and not url.startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
        try:
            url = request.build_absolute_uri(url)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return url


def _doc_public(doc, request=None):
    if doc is None:
        return None
    url = _doc_url(doc, request)
    if not url:
        return None
    return {
        'id': str(doc.id),
        'name': doc.name or '',
        'mime_type': doc.mime_type or '',
        'file_url': url,
    }


def _load_docs_map(doc_ids):
    ids = [str(x) for x in (doc_ids or []) if x]
    if not ids:
        return {}
    from apps.documents.models import Document
    docs = Document.objects.filter(id__in=ids).only(
        'id', 'name', 'mime_type', 'file',
    )
    return {str(d.id): d for d in docs}


def _expand_options(options, request=None):
    if not options:
        return options

    if isinstance(options, dict):
        columns = options.get('columns') or []
        ids = [
            c.get('image_document_id')
            for c in columns
            if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('image_document_id')
        ]
        docs_by_id = _load_docs_map(ids)
        new_columns = []
        for c in columns:
            if not isinstance(c, dict):
                continue
            entry = {
                'text': c.get('text', ''),
                'image_document_id': c.get('image_document_id'),
                'image_url': None,
                'image_name': None,
            }
            doc_id = c.get('image_document_id')
            if doc_id and str(doc_id) in docs_by_id:
                d = docs_by_id[str(doc_id)]
                url = _doc_url(d, request)
                if url:
                    entry['image_url'] = url
                    entry['image_name'] = d.name or ''
            new_columns.append(entry)
        return {
            'rows': list(options.get('rows') or []),
            'columns': new_columns,
        }

    if isinstance(options, list):
        ids = [
            o.get('image_document_id')
            for o in options
            if isinstance(o, dict) and o.get('image_document_id')
        ]
        docs_by_id = _load_docs_map(ids)
        result = []
        for o in options:
            if not isinstance(o, dict):
                continue
            entry = {
                'text': o.get('text', ''),
                'image_document_id': o.get('image_document_id'),
                'image_url': None,
                'image_name': None,
            }
            doc_id = o.get('image_document_id')
            if doc_id and str(doc_id) in docs_by_id:
                d = docs_by_id[str(doc_id)]
                url = _doc_url(d, request)
                if url:
                    entry['image_url'] = url
                    entry['image_name'] = d.name or ''
            result.append(entry)
        return result

    return options


def _workspace_avatar_url(briefing: Briefing, request=None) -> str:
    ws = getattr(briefing, 'workspace', None)
    if ws is not None:
        file_field = (
            getattr(ws, 'logo', None)
            or getattr(ws, 'avatar', None)
        )
        if file_field:
            try:
                url = file_field.url
            except Exception:
                url = ''
            if url:
                if request and not url.startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
                    try:
                        url = request.build_absolute_uri(url)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                return url

    owner = getattr(briefing, 'created_by', None)
    if owner is not None:
        try:
            from allauth.socialaccount.models import SocialAccount
            sa = (
                SocialAccount.objects
                .filter(user=owner, provider='google')
                .only('extra_data')
                .first()
            )
            if sa and isinstance(sa.extra_data, dict):
                pic = sa.extra_data.get('picture')
                if pic:
                    return pic
        except Exception:
            pass

    return ''


def _base_url(request):
    if request is not None:
        try:
            return request.build_absolute_uri('/').rstrip('/')
        except Exception:
            pass
    from django.conf import settings
    return getattr(settings, 'FRONTEND_URL', '').rstrip('/')


def _portal_briefing_url(briefing, request=None):
    base = _base_url(request)
    if not base:
        return ''
    return f'{base}/portal/briefings/{briefing.id}/'


def _client_page_url(briefing, request=None):
    base = _base_url(request)
    if not base:
        return ''
    return f'{base}/portal/briefings/{briefing.id}/respostas/'


def _client_pdf_url(briefing, request=None):
    base = _base_url(request)
    if not base:
        return ''
    return f'{base}/api/portal/briefings/{briefing.id}/export-pdf/'


def _architect_page_url(briefing, request=None):
    base = _base_url(request)
    if not base:
        return ''
    return f'{base}/w/{briefing.workspace_id}/briefings/{briefing.id}/resumo/'


def _architect_pdf_url(briefing, request=None):
    base = _base_url(request)
    if not base:
        return ''
    return f'{base}/api/briefings/{briefing.id}/export-pdf/'


class PortalBriefingSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    """Envelope do briefing para o portal. Dado bruto, sem análise."""

    def to_representation(self, briefing: Briefing):
        from apps.briefings.services.briefing_service import BriefingService

        request = self.context.get('request') if self.context else None

        answers = {
            str(a.question_id): a
            for a in briefing.answers.select_related(
                'latest_audio',
            ).prefetch_related('attachments')
        }

        sections = []
        ordered_sections = sorted(
            briefing.structure_snapshot.get('sections', []),
            key=lambda s: s.get('position', 0),
        )
        for section in ordered_sections:
            questions_payload = []
            ordered_questions = sorted(
                section.get('questions', []),
                key=lambda q: q.get('position', 0),
            )
            for q in ordered_questions:
                qid = str(q['id'])
                answer = answers.get(qid)

                latest_audio_url = ''
                if answer and answer.latest_audio and answer.latest_audio.file:
                    try:
                        latest_audio_url = answer.latest_audio.file.url
                    except Exception:
                        latest_audio_url = ''

                ref_docs = []
                ref_ids = list(q.get('reference_document_ids') or [])
                if ref_ids:
                    docs = _load_docs_map(ref_ids)
                    for rid in ref_ids:
                        d = docs.get(str(rid))
                        if d is None:
                            continue
                        payload = _doc_public(d, request)
                        if payload:
                            ref_docs.append(payload)

                attachments = []
                if answer is not None:
                    for doc in answer.attachments.all():
                        payload = _doc_public(doc, request)
                        if payload:
                            attachments.append(payload)

                expanded_options = _expand_options(
                    q.get('options'), request,
                )

                questions_payload.append({
                    'id': qid,
                    'text': q.get('text', ''),
                    'instructions': q.get('instructions', ''),
                    'question_type': q.get('question_type', 'text'),
                    'allowed_response_formats': list(
                        q.get('allowed_response_formats', [])
                    ),
                    'is_required': bool(q.get('is_required', True)),
                    'position': q.get('position', 0),
                    'is_custom': bool(q.get('is_custom', False)),
                    'reference_documents': ref_docs,
                    'options': expanded_options,
                    'state': (
                        answer.state if answer
                        else BriefingAnswer.STATE_PENDING
                    ),
                    'latest_content': answer.latest_content if answer else '',
                    'latest_audio_url': latest_audio_url,
                    'latest_transcription': (
                        answer.latest_transcription if answer else ''
                    ),
                    'transcription_status': (
                        answer.transcription_status if answer
                        else BriefingAnswer.TRANSCRIPTION_NOT_APPLICABLE
                    ),
                    'attachments': attachments,
                })
            sections.append({
                'id': section.get('id'),
                'title': section.get('title', ''),
                'description': section.get('description', ''),
                'position': section.get('position', 0),
                'questions': questions_payload,
            })

        next_q = BriefingService.get_next_eligible_question(briefing)

        workspace_name = ''
        if briefing.workspace is not None:
            workspace_name = briefing.workspace.name or ''

        client_name = ''
        if briefing.client is not None:
            client_name = briefing.client.name or ''

        return {
            'id': str(briefing.id),
            'title': briefing.title,
            'workspace_id': (
                str(briefing.workspace_id)
                if briefing.workspace_id else ''
            ),
            'workspace_name': workspace_name,
            'workspace_avatar_url': _workspace_avatar_url(briefing, request),
            'client_id': (
                str(briefing.client_id) if briefing.client_id else ''
            ),
            'client_name': client_name,
            'intro_message': briefing.intro_message or '',
            'show_intro': briefing.show_intro_to_portal(),
            'portal_url': _portal_briefing_url(briefing, request),
            'briefing_page_url': _client_page_url(briefing, request),
            'briefing_pdf_url': _client_pdf_url(briefing, request),
            'briefing_architect_url': _architect_page_url(briefing, request),
            'briefing_architect_pdf_url': _architect_pdf_url(briefing, request),
            'status': briefing.status,
            'portal_status': briefing.portal_status(),
            'can_answer': briefing.portal_can_answer(),
            'expires_at': (
                briefing.expires_at.isoformat()
                if briefing.expires_at else None
            ),
            'first_opened_at': (
                briefing.first_opened_at.isoformat()
                if briefing.first_opened_at else None
            ),
            'progress': BriefingService.compute_progress(briefing),
            'next_question_id': str(next_q['id']) if next_q else None,
            'has_custom_questions': bool(briefing.has_custom_questions),
            'sections': sections,
        }

class PortalAnswerSubmitSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    text = serializers.CharField(
        required=False, allow_blank=True, max_length=20000,
    )
    audio_document_id = serializers.UUIDField(
        required=False, allow_null=True,
    )
    attachment_ids = serializers.ListField(
        child=serializers.UUIDField(),
        required=False, allow_empty=True, default=list,
    )

    def validate(self, attrs):
        text = (attrs.get('text') or '').strip()
        has_audio = attrs.get('audio_document_id') is not None
        has_attachments = bool(attrs.get('attachment_ids'))
        if not text and not has_audio and not has_attachments:
            raise serializers.ValidationError(
                'Envie um texto, um áudio ou uma imagem.'
            )
        attrs['text'] = text
        return attrs

"""Serializers de BriefingQuestionInstance."""

from rest_framework import serializers

from apps.briefings.models import BriefingQuestionInstance


class BriefingQuestionInstanceSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = BriefingQuestionInstance
        fields = [
            'id',
            'briefing',
            'section_ref',
            'text',
            'instructions',
            'question_type',
            'allowed_response_formats',
            'options',
            'position',
            'is_required',
            'created_by',
            'created_at',
            'updated_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields


def _validate_choice_options(value):
    if not isinstance(value, list):
        raise serializers.ValidationError('Deve ser uma lista.')
    cleaned = []
    for item in value:
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            continue
        text = (item.get('text') or '').strip()
        if not text:
            continue
        cleaned.append({
            'text': text,
            'image_document_id': item.get('image_document_id') or None,
        })
    return cleaned


def _validate_grid_options(value):
    if not isinstance(value, dict):
        raise serializers.ValidationError(
            'Para grades, deve ser um dict com "rows" e "columns".'
        )
    rows = value.get('rows') or []
    columns = value.get('columns') or []
    if not isinstance(rows, list) or not isinstance(columns, list):
        raise serializers.ValidationError(
            'Grid precisa de "rows" (list) e "columns" (list).'
        )
    clean_rows = [str(r).strip() for r in rows if str(r).strip()]
    clean_columns = []
    for col in columns:
        if isinstance(col, str):
            text = col.strip()
            if text:
                clean_columns.append({'text': text, 'image_document_id': None})
        elif isinstance(col, dict):
            text = (col.get('text') or '').strip()
            if text:
                clean_columns.append({
                    'text': text,
                    'image_document_id': col.get('image_document_id') or None,
                })
    return {'rows': clean_rows, 'columns': clean_columns}


class BriefingQuestionInstanceCreateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    section_ref = serializers.CharField(max_length=36)
    text = serializers.CharField(max_length=2000, allow_blank=False)
    instructions = serializers.CharField(
        required=False, allow_blank=True, max_length=2000,
    )
    question_type = serializers.CharField(
        max_length=30, required=False, default='text',
    )
    allowed_response_formats = serializers.ListField(
        child=serializers.CharField(max_length=20),
        required=False,
        allow_empty=False,
    )
    options = serializers.JSONField(required=False, default=list)
    is_required = serializers.BooleanField(required=False, default=True)

    def validate_text(self, value):
        value = (value or '').strip()
        if not value:
            raise serializers.ValidationError('O enunciado é obrigatório.')
        return value

    def validate(self, attrs):
        q_type = attrs.get('question_type') or 'text'
        options = attrs.get('options')
        if q_type in ('grid_single', 'grid_multiple') and options is not None:
            attrs['options'] = _validate_grid_options(options)
        elif options is not None:
            attrs['options'] = _validate_choice_options(options)
        return attrs


class BriefingQuestionInstanceUpdateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    text = serializers.CharField(
        required=False, allow_blank=False, max_length=2000,
    )
    instructions = serializers.CharField(
        required=False, allow_blank=True, max_length=2000,
    )
    question_type = serializers.CharField(
        max_length=30, required=False,
    )
    allowed_response_formats = serializers.ListField(
        child=serializers.CharField(max_length=20),
        required=False,
        allow_empty=False,
    )
    options = serializers.JSONField(required=False)
    is_required = serializers.BooleanField(required=False)

    def validate_text(self, value):
        value = (value or '').strip()
        if not value:
            raise serializers.ValidationError('O enunciado não pode ser vazio.')
        return value

    def validate(self, attrs):
        q_type = attrs.get('question_type')
        options = attrs.get('options')
        if options is None:
            return attrs
        # Se o tipo não foi enviado, valida como lista (comportamento
        # antigo). Se foi enviado e é grid, valida como dict.
        if q_type in ('grid_single', 'grid_multiple'):
            attrs['options'] = _validate_grid_options(options)
        elif q_type is None:
            # Não sabemos o tipo. Aceita qualquer forma — a
            # validação acontecerá no model na hora de salvar.
            pass
        else:
            attrs['options'] = _validate_choice_options(options)
        return attrs

"""
Serializers de BriefingTemplate, BriefingSection, BriefingQuestion
e BriefingTemplateEvent.

REGRA DE URL
-----------
Toda URL de mídia retornada por serializers passa por `_absolute_url`.
"""

from rest_framework import serializers

from apps.briefings.models import (
    BriefingTemplate,
    BriefingSection,
    BriefingQuestion,
    BriefingTemplateEvent,
)


def _absolute_url(request, url):
    if not url:
        return ''
    if url.startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
        return url
    if request is not None:
        try:
            return request.build_absolute_uri(url)
        except Exception:
            return url
    return url


def _validate_choice_options(value):
    if not isinstance(value, list):
        raise serializers.ValidationError('Deve ser uma lista.')

    cleaned = []
    for item in value:
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            continue
        text = (item.get('text') or '').strip()
        if not text:
            continue
        image_id = item.get('image_document_id') or None
        cleaned.append({
            'text': text,
            'image_document_id': image_id,
        })
    return cleaned


def _validate_grid_options(value):
    if not isinstance(value, dict):
        raise serializers.ValidationError(
            'Para grades, deve ser um dict com "rows" e "columns".'
        )

    rows = value.get('rows') or []
    columns = value.get('columns') or []

    if not isinstance(rows, list) or not isinstance(columns, list):
        raise serializers.ValidationError(
            'Grid precisa de "rows" (list) e "columns" (list).'
        )

    clean_rows = [str(r).strip() for r in rows if str(r).strip()]

    clean_columns = []
    for col in columns:
        if isinstance(col, str):
            text = col.strip()
            if text:
                clean_columns.append({
                    'text': text,
                    'image_document_id': None,
                })
        elif isinstance(col, dict):
            text = (col.get('text') or '').strip()
            if text:
                clean_columns.append({
                    'text': text,
                    'image_document_id': col.get('image_document_id') or None,
                })

    return {
        'rows': clean_rows,
        'columns': clean_columns,
    }


class BriefingQuestionSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    reference_document_ids = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    options = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = BriefingQuestion
        fields = [
            'id', 'text', 'instructions', 'question_type',
            'allowed_response_formats', 'options', 'position',
            'is_required', 'reference_document_ids',
            'created_at', 'updated_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields

    def get_reference_document_ids(self, obj):
        return [str(d.id) for d in obj.reference_documents.all()]

    def get_options(self, obj):
        raw_options = obj.options or []

        if isinstance(raw_options, dict):
            raw_columns = raw_options.get('columns') or []
            doc_ids = [
                str(col.get('image_document_id'))
                for col in raw_columns
                if isinstance(col, dict) and col.get('image_document_id')
            ]
            docs_by_id = self._load_documents_map(doc_ids)
            request = self.context.get('request')

            clean_columns = []
            for col in raw_columns:
                if not isinstance(col, dict):
                    continue
                entry = {
                    'text': col.get('text', ''),
                    'image_document_id': col.get('image_document_id'),
                    'image_url': None,
                }
                doc_id = col.get('image_document_id')
                if doc_id:
                    doc = docs_by_id.get(str(doc_id))
                    if doc and doc.file:
                        try:
                            entry['image_url'] = _absolute_url(
                                request, doc.file.url,
                            )
                        except Exception:
                            entry['image_url'] = None
                clean_columns.append(entry)

            return {
                'rows': list(raw_options.get('rows') or []),
                'columns': clean_columns,
            }

        if not isinstance(raw_options, list) or not raw_options:
            return []

        doc_ids = [
            str(opt.get('image_document_id'))
            for opt in raw_options
            if isinstance(opt, dict) and opt.get('image_document_id')
        ]
        docs_by_id = self._load_documents_map(doc_ids)
        request = self.context.get('request')

        result = []
        for opt in raw_options:
            if not isinstance(opt, dict):
                continue
            entry = {
                'text': opt.get('text', ''),
                'image_document_id': opt.get('image_document_id'),
                'image_url': None,
            }
            doc_id = opt.get('image_document_id')
            if doc_id:
                doc = docs_by_id.get(str(doc_id))
                if doc and doc.file:
                    try:
                        entry['image_url'] = _absolute_url(
                            request, doc.file.url,
                        )
                    except Exception:
                        entry['image_url'] = None
            result.append(entry)
        return result

    @staticmethod
    def _load_documents_map(doc_ids):
        if not doc_ids:
            return {}
        from apps.documents.models import Document
        docs = Document.objects.filter(id__in=doc_ids)
        return {str(d.id): d for d in docs}


class BriefingQuestionCreateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    text = serializers.CharField(max_length=2000, allow_blank=False)
    instructions = serializers.CharField(
        required=False, allow_blank=True, max_length=2000,
    )
    question_type = serializers.ChoiceField(
        choices=[c[0] for c in BriefingQuestion.QUESTION_TYPE_CHOICES],
        required=False,
        default=BriefingQuestion.QUESTION_TYPE_TEXT,
    )
    allowed_response_formats = serializers.ListField(
        child=serializers.ChoiceField(choices=[
            BriefingQuestion.RESPONSE_FORMAT_TEXT,
            BriefingQuestion.RESPONSE_FORMAT_AUDIO,
            BriefingQuestion.RESPONSE_FORMAT_IMAGE,
        ]),
        required=False,
        allow_empty=False,
    )
    options = serializers.JSONField(required=False)
    is_required = serializers.BooleanField(required=False, default=True)
    reference_document_ids = serializers.ListField(
        child=serializers.UUIDField(),
        required=False,
        allow_empty=True,
        default=list,
    )

    def validate_text(self, value):
        value = (value or '').strip()
        if not value:
            raise serializers.ValidationError('O enunciado é obrigatório.')
        return value

    def validate(self, attrs):
        q_type = attrs.get('question_type') or BriefingQuestion.QUESTION_TYPE_TEXT
        raw_options = attrs.get('options')

        if raw_options is None:
            attrs['options'] = []
            return attrs

        if q_type in BriefingQuestion.GRID_TYPES:
            attrs['options'] = _validate_grid_options(raw_options)
        elif q_type in BriefingQuestion.CHOICE_TYPES:
            attrs['options'] = _validate_choice_options(raw_options)
        else:
            attrs['options'] = []

        return attrs


class BriefingQuestionUpdateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    text = serializers.CharField(
        required=False, allow_blank=False, max_length=2000,
    )
    instructions = serializers.CharField(
        required=False, allow_blank=True, max_length=2000,
    )
    question_type = serializers.ChoiceField(
        choices=[c[0] for c in BriefingQuestion.QUESTION_TYPE_CHOICES],
        required=False,
    )
    allowed_response_formats = serializers.ListField(
        child=serializers.ChoiceField(choices=[
            BriefingQuestion.RESPONSE_FORMAT_TEXT,
            BriefingQuestion.RESPONSE_FORMAT_AUDIO,
            BriefingQuestion.RESPONSE_FORMAT_IMAGE,
        ]),
        required=False,
        allow_empty=False,
    )
    options = serializers.JSONField(required=False)
    is_required = serializers.BooleanField(required=False)
    reference_document_ids = serializers.ListField(
        child=serializers.UUIDField(),
        required=False,
        allow_empty=True,
    )

    def validate_text(self, value):
        value = (value or '').strip()
        if not value:
            raise serializers.ValidationError('O enunciado não pode ser vazio.')
        return value

    def validate(self, attrs):
        q_type = attrs.get('question_type')
        raw_options = attrs.get('options')

        if raw_options is None:
            return attrs

        if q_type in BriefingQuestion.GRID_TYPES:
            attrs['options'] = _validate_grid_options(raw_options)
        elif q_type in BriefingQuestion.CHOICE_TYPES:
            attrs['options'] = _validate_choice_options(raw_options)
        elif q_type is None:
            if isinstance(raw_options, dict):
                attrs['options'] = _validate_grid_options(raw_options)
            else:
                attrs['options'] = _validate_choice_options(raw_options)
        else:
            attrs['options'] = []

        return attrs

class BriefingSectionCreateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    title = serializers.CharField(max_length=200, allow_blank=False)
    description = serializers.CharField(
        required=False, allow_blank=True, max_length=2000,
    )

    def validate_title(self, value):
        value = (value or '').strip()
        if not value:
            raise serializers.ValidationError('O título é obrigatório.')
        return value

class BriefingTemplateListSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    sections_count = serializers.IntegerField(read_only=True)
    questions_count = serializers.IntegerField(read_only=True)
    status_display = serializers.CharField(
        source='get_status_display', read_only=True,
    )
    cover_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    category_id = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = BriefingTemplate
        fields = [
            'id', 'name', 'description', 'category', 'category_id',
            'status', 'status_display', 'position', 'cover_url',
            'intro_message', 'sections_count', 'questions_count',
            'created_at', 'updated_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields

    def get_cover_url(self, obj):
        if not obj.cover:
            return ''
        try:
            url = obj.cover.url
        except Exception:
            return ''
        request = self.context.get('request')
        return _absolute_url(request, url)

    def get_category_id(self, obj):
        if obj.category_fk_id:
            return str(obj.category_fk_id)
        return None

    sections = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    )

    can_start_execution = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    cover_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    category_id = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = BriefingTemplate
        fields = [
            'id', 'name', 'description', 'category', 'category_id',
            'cover_url', 'intro_message',
            'status', 'status_display', 'position',
            'is_editable', 'can_start_execution',
            'sections_count', 'questions_count', 'sections',
            'created_at', 'updated_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields

    def get_sections(self, obj):
        qs = obj.sections.prefetch_related(
            'questions__reference_documents',
        ).order_by('position', 'created_at')
        return BriefingSectionSerializer(
            qs, many=True, context=self.context,
        ).data

    def get_sections_count(self, obj):
        return obj.sections.count()

    def get_questions_count(self, obj):
        return BriefingQuestion.objects.filter(
            section__template=obj,
        ).count()

        return obj.is_editable()

    def get_can_start_execution(self, obj):
        return obj.can_start_execution()

    def get_cover_url(self, obj):
        if not obj.cover:
            return ''
        try:
            url = obj.cover.url
        except Exception:
            return ''
        request = self.context.get('request')
        return _absolute_url(request, url)

    def get_category_id(self, obj):
        if obj.category_fk_id:
            return str(obj.category_fk_id)
        return None

"""
Serializers de BriefingTemplateVersion.

REGRA:
- `BriefingTemplateVersionListSerializer` → metadados apenas (SEM `structure`).
  Usado por GET /api/briefing-templates/{id}/versions/.
- `BriefingTemplateVersionSerializer` → detalhe completo (COM `structure`).
  Usado por GET /api/briefing-templates/{id}/versions/{vid}/.

O cliente busca o detalhe sob demanda ao clicar em uma versão.

CACHE:
- O backend NÃO define `Cache-Control` aqui. A decisão de cachear é do
  cliente (browser/JS). Se futuramente forem adicionados cabeçalhos,
  ATENÇÃO: uma versão `published` é imutável, mas uma versão `draft`
  pode mudar. Não trate as duas igualmente.
"""

from rest_framework import serializers

from apps.briefings.models import BriefingTemplateVersion


# =========================================================================
# LIST — metadados apenas
# =========================================================================

class BriefingTemplateVersionListSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    status_display = serializers.CharField(
        source='get_status_display', read_only=True,
    )
    sections_count = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    questions_count = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = BriefingTemplateVersion
        fields = [
            'id',
            'template',
            'version_number',
            'status',
            'status_display',
            'sections_count',
            'questions_count',
            'published_at',
            'created_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields

    def get_sections_count(self, obj):
        s = (obj.structure or {}).get('sections') or []
        return len(s)

    def get_questions_count(self, obj):
        s = (obj.structure or {}).get('sections') or []
        total = 0
        for sec in s:
            total += len(sec.get('questions') or [])
        return total


# =========================================================================
# DETAIL — estrutura completa
# =========================================================================

class BriefingTemplateVersionSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    status_display = serializers.CharField(
        source='get_status_display', read_only=True,
    )
    is_editable = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    sections_count = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    questions_count = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = BriefingTemplateVersion
        fields = [
            'id',
            'template',
            'version_number',
            'status',
            'status_display',
            'is_editable',
            'structure',
            'sections_count',
            'questions_count',
            'published_at',
            'created_by',
            'created_at',
            'updated_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields

    def get_is_editable(self, obj):
        return obj.is_editable()

    def get_sections_count(self, obj):
        s = (obj.structure or {}).get('sections') or []
        return len(s)

    def get_questions_count(self, obj):
        s = (obj.structure or {}).get('sections') or []
        total = 0
        for sec in s:
            total += len(sec.get('questions') or [])
        return total


# =========================================================================
# CREATE — body do POST /versions/
# =========================================================================

class BriefingTemplateVersionCreateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    """
    Body do POST /api/briefing-templates/<id>/versions/.

    Modos:
        'clone_current'          → clona a estrutura atual das tabelas (default)
        'blank'                  → cria rascunho vazio
        'restore_from_version'   → cria rascunho a partir de uma versão
                                   existente (usa `source_version_id`)
    """
    mode = serializers.ChoiceField(
        choices=['clone_current', 'blank', 'restore_from_version'],
        required=False,
        default='clone_current',
    )
    source_version_id = serializers.UUIDField(
        required=False,
        allow_null=True,
    )

    def validate(self, attrs):
        mode = attrs.get('mode', 'clone_current')
        source_id = attrs.get('source_version_id')
        if mode == 'restore_from_version' and not source_id:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({
                'source_version_id': (
                    'Obrigatório quando mode="restore_from_version".'
                ),
            })
        return attrs

"""
BriefingViewSet — CRUD + ações do arquiteto sobre execuções.
"""

import logging

from django.http import FileResponse
from rest_framework import status, viewsets
from rest_framework.decorators import action
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response

from apps.briefings.api.serializers import (
    BriefingListSerializer,
    BriefingDetailSerializer,
    BriefingCreateSerializer,
    BriefingReopenSerializer,
    BriefingEventSerializer,
    BriefingNoteSerializer,
    BriefingNoteCreateSerializer,
    BriefingQuestionInstanceSerializer,
    BriefingQuestionInstanceCreateSerializer,
    BriefingQuestionInstanceUpdateSerializer,
    BriefingTemplateVersionSerializer,
)
from apps.briefings.services import (
    BriefingService,
    BriefingTemplateService,
    BriefingNoteService,
    BriefingSendService,
    BriefingQuestionInstanceService,
    BriefingTemplateVersionService,
)
from apps.briefings.services.export_service import BriefingExportService
from apps.briefings.exceptions import (
    BriefingError,
    BriefingNotFoundError,
    BriefingReopenError,
    BriefingClosedError,
    BriefingExpiredError,
    BriefingPausedError,
    BriefingNoteNotFoundError,
)
from apps.clients.models import Client
from apps.workspaces.queries import WorkspaceQueries
from apps.workspaces.models import WorkspaceMembership
from apps.console.models import ConsoleGovernanceCapability

logger = logging.getLogger('briefings')


class BriefingViewSet(viewsets.GenericViewSet):

    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    lookup_field = 'id'

    def _current_workspace(self):
        workspace_id = self.request.session.get('current_workspace_id')
        if not workspace_id:
            return None
        return WorkspaceQueries.get_for_user(workspace_id, self.request.user)

    def _require_capability(self, workspace, capability_code):
        membership = WorkspaceMembership.objects.filter(
            workspace=workspace,
            user=self.request.user,
        ).select_related('governance').first()

        if membership is None:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Você não é membro deste Workspace'},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            )
        if membership.governance.key == 'proprietario':
            return None
        has = ConsoleGovernanceCapability.objects.filter(
            governance=membership.governance,
            capability__code=capability_code,
            capability__is_active=True,
        ).exists()
        if not has:
            return Response(
                {'error': f'Sua governança não permite esta ação ({capability_code}).'},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            )
        return None

    @staticmethod
    def _handle_domain_error(exc):
        if isinstance(exc, BriefingNotFoundError):
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=404)
        if isinstance(exc, BriefingReopenError):
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=409)
        if isinstance(exc, BriefingClosedError):
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=409)
        if isinstance(exc, BriefingExpiredError):
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=409)
        if isinstance(exc, BriefingPausedError):
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=409)
        if isinstance(exc, BriefingNoteNotFoundError):
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=404)
        if isinstance(exc, BriefingError):
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=400)
        return None

    def list(self, request):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.briefing.view')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        status_filter = request.query_params.get('status')
        client_id = request.query_params.get('client')

        qs = workspace.briefings.select_related(
            'client', 'template', 'template_version',
        ).order_by('-created_at')
        if status_filter:
            qs = qs.filter(status=status_filter)
        if client_id:
            qs = qs.filter(client_id=client_id)

        return Response(BriefingListSerializer(qs, many=True).data)

    def create(self, request):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.briefing.start')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        serializer = BriefingCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            template = BriefingTemplateService.get_or_raise(
                serializer.validated_data['template_id'], workspace,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Template não encontrado'}, status=404)

        client_id = serializer.validated_data['client_id']
        client = Client.objects.filter(
            id=client_id, workspace=workspace,
        ).first()
        if client is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Cliente não encontrado'}, status=404)

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.create_briefing(
                template=template,
                client=client,
                workspace=workspace,
                actor=request.user,
                expires_at=serializer.validated_data.get('expires_at'),
                title=serializer.validated_data.get('title') or None,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao criar briefing')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingDetailSerializer(briefing).data,
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )

    def retrieve(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.briefing.view')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.get_or_raise(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        return Response(BriefingDetailSerializer(briefing).data)

    def destroy(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.briefing.delete')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.get_or_raise(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        briefing.delete()
        return Response(status=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='pause')
    def pause(self, request, id=None):
        return self._simple_action(id, request, 'pause')

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='resume')
    def resume(self, request, id=None):
        return self._simple_action(id, request, 'resume')

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='close')
    def close(self, request, id=None):
        return self._simple_action(id, request, 'close')

    def _simple_action(self, briefing_id, request, name):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.briefing.review')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.get_or_raise(briefing_id, workspace)
            method = getattr(BriefingService, name)
            briefing = method(briefing=briefing, actor=request.user)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro em ação %s', name)
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(BriefingDetailSerializer(briefing).data)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='reopen')
    def reopen(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.briefing.review')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.get_or_raise(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        serializer = BriefingReopenSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.reopen(
                briefing=briefing,
                actor=request.user,
                new_expires_at=serializer.validated_data.get('new_expires_at'),
                reason=serializer.validated_data.get('reason', ''),
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao reabrir briefing')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(BriefingDetailSerializer(briefing).data)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['get'], url_path='events')
    def events(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.briefing.view')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.get_or_raise(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        qs = briefing.events.select_related(
            'user', 'portal_session',
        ).order_by('-created_at')[:500]
        return Response(BriefingEventSerializer(qs, many=True).data)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['get', 'post'], url_path='notes')
    def notes(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)

        if request.method == 'GET':
            err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.note.view')
            if err is not None:
                return err

            try:
                briefing = BriefingService.get_or_raise(id, workspace)
            except Exception as exc:
                handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
                if handled is not None:
                    return handled
                return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

            qs = BriefingNoteService.list_for_briefing(briefing)
            return Response(BriefingNoteSerializer(qs, many=True).data)

        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.note.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.get_or_raise(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        serializer = BriefingNoteCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            note = BriefingNoteService.create_note(
                briefing=briefing,
                author=request.user,
                content=serializer.validated_data['content'],
                question_id=serializer.validated_data.get('question_id'),
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao criar anotação')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingNoteSerializer(note).data,
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )

    @action(
        detail=True, methods=['patch', 'delete'],
        url_path=r'notes/(?P<note_id>[^/.]+)',
    )
    def note_detail(self, request, id=None, note_id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.note.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.get_or_raise(id, workspace)
            note = BriefingNoteService.get_or_raise(note_id, briefing)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        if request.method == 'DELETE':
            BriefingNoteService.delete_note(note=note)
            return Response(status=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)

        serializer = BriefingNoteCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            note = BriefingNoteService.update_note(
                note=note,
                author=request.user,
                content=serializer.validated_data['content'],
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao editar anotação')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(BriefingNoteSerializer(note).data)

    @action(
        detail=True,
        methods=['get', 'post'],
        url_path='custom-questions',
    )
    def custom_questions(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)

        if request.method == 'GET':
            err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.briefing.view')
            if err is not None:
                return err
        else:
            err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.briefing.review')
            if err is not None:
                return err

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.get_or_raise(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        if request.method == 'GET':
            qs = BriefingQuestionInstanceService.list_for_briefing(briefing)
            return Response(
                BriefingQuestionInstanceSerializer(qs, many=True).data
            )

        serializer = BriefingQuestionInstanceCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            instance = BriefingQuestionInstanceService.add_custom_question(
                briefing=briefing,
                section_ref=serializer.validated_data['section_ref'],
                text=serializer.validated_data['text'],
                instructions=serializer.validated_data.get('instructions', ''),
                question_type=serializer.validated_data.get(
                    'question_type', 'text',
                ),
                allowed_response_formats=serializer.validated_data.get(
                    'allowed_response_formats',
                ),
                options=serializer.validated_data.get('options'),
                is_required=serializer.validated_data.get('is_required', True),
                actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao criar pergunta exclusiva')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingQuestionInstanceSerializer(instance).data,
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )

    @action(
        detail=True,
        methods=['patch', 'delete'],
        url_path=r'custom-questions/(?P<instance_id>[^/.]+)',
    )
    def custom_question_detail(self, request, id=None, instance_id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.briefing.review')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.get_or_raise(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        try:
            instance = BriefingQuestionInstanceService.get_or_raise(
                instance_id, briefing,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        if request.method == 'DELETE':
            try:
                BriefingQuestionInstanceService.remove_custom_question(
                    instance=instance, actor=request.user,
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
                if handled is not None:
                    return handled
                logger.exception('Erro ao remover pergunta exclusiva')
                return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)
            return Response(status=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)

        serializer = BriefingQuestionInstanceUpdateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            instance = BriefingQuestionInstanceService.update_custom_question(
                instance=instance,
                text=serializer.validated_data.get('text'),
                instructions=serializer.validated_data.get('instructions'),
                question_type=serializer.validated_data.get('question_type'),
                allowed_response_formats=serializer.validated_data.get(
                    'allowed_response_formats',
                ),
                options=serializer.validated_data.get('options'),
                is_required=serializer.validated_data.get('is_required'),
                actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao editar pergunta exclusiva')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(BriefingQuestionInstanceSerializer(instance).data)

    @action(
        detail=True,
        methods=['post'],
        url_path='promote-to-template-version',
    )
    def promote_to_template_version(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.get_or_raise(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        if briefing.template_id is None:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Este briefing não tem modelo de origem. '
                          'Não é possível criar uma nova versão.'},
                status=400,
            )

        include_custom = request.data.get('include_custom_questions', True)

        try:
            version = (
                BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_briefing(
                    briefing=briefing,
                    actor=request.user,
                    include_custom_questions=bool(include_custom),
                    template=briefing.template,
                )
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao promover briefing a versão')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingTemplateVersionSerializer(version).data,
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )

    @action(detail=True, methods=['get'], url_path='export-pdf')
    def export_pdf(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.briefing.export')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.get_or_raise(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        try:
            pdf_bytes, filename = BriefingExportService.render_pdf(
                briefing, request=request, mode='architect',
            )
        except RuntimeError as exc:
            logger.warning(
                'Falha ao gerar PDF do arquiteto (briefing=%s): %s',
                briefing.id, exc,
            )
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=500)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception('Falha inesperada ao gerar PDF do arquiteto')
            return Response(
                {'error': f'Erro inesperado: {exc}'},
                status=500,
            )

        from io import BytesIO
        return FileResponse(
            BytesIO(pdf_bytes),
            as_attachment=True,
            filename=filename,
            content_type='application/pdf',
        )

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='send-to-client')
    def send_to_client(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(
            workspace, 'briefings.briefing.send_to_client',
        )
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            briefing = BriefingService.get_or_raise(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        to_email = (request.data.get('to_email') or '').strip() or None
        message = (request.data.get('message') or '').strip()

        try:
            result = BriefingSendService.send_to_client(
                briefing=briefing,
                actor=request.user,
                to_email=to_email,
                message=message,
                request=request,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao enviar briefing manualmente')
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Falha ao enviar o briefing.'},
                status=500,
            )

        return Response(result, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
