"""
BriefingTemplateCategoryViewSet — CRUD de categorias de modelo.
"""

from rest_framework import viewsets, status
from rest_framework.decorators import action
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response

from apps.workspaces.queries import WorkspaceQueries
from apps.briefings.models.template_category import BriefingTemplateCategory


class BriefingTemplateCategoryViewSet(viewsets.GenericViewSet):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    lookup_field = 'id'

    def _current_workspace(self):
        ws_id = self.request.session.get('current_workspace_id')
        if not ws_id:
            return None
        return WorkspaceQueries.get_for_user(ws_id, self.request.user)

    def list(self, request):
        ws = self._current_workspace()
        if ws is None:
            return Response([], status=200)
        qs = ws.briefing_template_categories.all().order_by('position', 'name')
        data = [
            {'id': str(c.id), 'name': c.name, 'position': c.position}
            for c in qs
        ]
        return Response(data)

    def create(self, request):
        ws = self._current_workspace()
        if ws is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)

        name = (request.data.get('name') or '').strip()
        if not name:
            return Response({'error': 'Nome obrigatório'}, status=400)

        if BriefingTemplateCategory.objects.filter(
            workspace=ws, name__iexact=name,
        ).exists():
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Já existe uma categoria com esse nome.'},
                status=400,
            )

        last = (
            BriefingTemplateCategory.objects
            .filter(workspace=ws)
            .order_by('-position')
            .first()
        )
        cat = BriefingTemplateCategory.objects.create(
            workspace=ws,
            name=name,
            position=(last.position + 1 if last else 1),
        )
        return Response(
            {'id': str(cat.id), 'name': cat.name, 'position': cat.position},
            status=201,
        )

    def partial_update(self, request, id=None):
        ws = self._current_workspace()
        if ws is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)

        cat = ws.briefing_template_categories.filter(id=id).first()
        if not cat:
            return Response({'error': 'Não encontrada'}, status=404)

        name = (request.data.get('name') or '').strip()
        if name:
            cat.name = name
            cat.save(update_fields=['name', 'updated_at'])

        return Response({
            'id': str(cat.id), 'name': cat.name, 'position': cat.position,
        })

    def destroy(self, request, id=None):
        ws = self._current_workspace()
        if ws is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)

        cat = ws.briefing_template_categories.filter(id=id).first()
        if not cat:
            return Response({'error': 'Não encontrada'}, status=404)

        cat.delete()
        return Response(status=204)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='reorder')
    def reorder(self, request):
        ws = self._current_workspace()
        if ws is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)

        ordered_ids = request.data.get('ordered_ids') or []
        if not isinstance(ordered_ids, list):
            return Response({'error': 'ordered_ids precisa ser lista'}, status=400)

        cats = {
            str(c.id): c
            for c in ws.briefing_template_categories.all()
        }

        for sid in ordered_ids:
            if str(sid) not in cats:
                return Response(
                    {'error': f'Categoria {sid} não pertence ao workspace.'},
                    status=400,
                )

        OFFSET = 100000
        for i, sid in enumerate(ordered_ids):
            c = cats[str(sid)]
            c.position = OFFSET + i
            c.save(update_fields=['position'])
        for i, sid in enumerate(ordered_ids):
            c = cats[str(sid)]
            c.position = i + 1
            c.save(update_fields=['position'])

        return Response({'ok': True})

"""
Views do Portal do Cliente.

Autenticação: ClientPortalSession (via ClientPortalMiddleware).
"""

import logging

from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.permissions import BasePermission
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.briefings.api.serializers import (
    PortalBriefingSerializer,
    PortalBriefingListSerializer,
    PortalAnswerSubmitSerializer,
    PortalClarificationSerializer,
    PortalReviewRequestSerializer,
)
from apps.briefings.services import BriefingService
from apps.briefings.exceptions import (
    BriefingError,
    BriefingNotFoundError,
    BriefingClosedError,
    BriefingExpiredError,
    BriefingPausedError,
    InvalidQuestionSequenceError,
    InvalidResponseFormatError,
)
from apps.briefings.queries import BriefingQueries
from apps.documents.models import Document

logger = logging.getLogger('briefings')


class IsPortalSessionAuthenticated(BasePermission):
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        session = getattr(request, 'portal_session', None)
        if session is None:
            return False
        return session.is_valid()


def _resolve_briefing_or_404(briefing_id, request):
    client = getattr(request, 'portal_client', None)
    if client is None:
        return None
    return BriefingQueries.get_for_client(client, briefing_id)


def _handle_error(exc, briefing=None):
    if isinstance(exc, BriefingNotFoundError):
        return Response(
            {'error': str(exc), 'code': 'not_found'}, status=404,
        )
    if isinstance(exc, BriefingPausedError):
        return Response(
            {'error': str(exc), 'code': 'paused'}, status=409,
        )
    if isinstance(exc, BriefingClosedError):
        return Response(
            {'error': str(exc), 'code': 'cancelled'}, status=409,
        )
    if isinstance(exc, BriefingExpiredError):
        return Response(
            {'error': str(exc), 'code': 'cancelled'}, status=409,
        )
    if isinstance(exc, InvalidQuestionSequenceError):
        expected_id = None
        if briefing is not None:
            try:
                next_q = BriefingService.get_next_eligible_question(briefing)
                if next_q:
                    expected_id = str(next_q['id'])
            except Exception:
                pass
        return Response(
            {
                'error': str(exc),
                'code': 'out_of_sequence',
                'expected_question_id': expected_id,
            },
            status=409,
        )
    if isinstance(exc, InvalidResponseFormatError):
        return Response(
            {'error': str(exc), 'code': 'invalid_format'}, status=400,
        )
    if isinstance(exc, BriefingError):
        return Response(
            {'error': str(exc), 'code': 'briefing_error'}, status=400,
        )
    return None


def _absolute_url(request, url):
    if not url:
        return ''
    if url.startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
        return url
    if request is not None:
        try:
            return request.build_absolute_uri(url)
        except Exception:
            return url
    return url


def _briefing_cover_url(briefing, request):
    template = briefing.template
    if template is None:
        return ''
    if not template.cover:
        return ''
    try:
        url = template.cover.url
    except Exception:
        return ''
    return _absolute_url(request, url)


def _serialize(briefing, request):
    return PortalBriefingSerializer(
        context={'request': request},
    ).to_representation(briefing)


class PortalBriefingListView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsPortalSessionAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        client = getattr(request, 'portal_client', None)
        if client is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Sessão inválida'}, status=401)

        qs = BriefingQueries.list_for_client(client)
        for b in qs:
            b.mark_expired_if_needed()
        qs = BriefingQueries.list_for_client(client)

        data = []
        for b in qs:
            item = PortalBriefingListSerializer().to_representation(b)
            item['cover_url'] = _briefing_cover_url(b, request)
            data.append(item)
        return Response(data)


class PortalBriefingView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsPortalSessionAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request, briefing_id=None):
        briefing = _resolve_briefing_or_404(briefing_id, request)
        if briefing is None:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Briefing não encontrado', 'code': 'not_found'},
                status=404,
            )

        briefing.mark_expired_if_needed()

        try:
            BriefingService.sync_snapshot_references(briefing)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception(
                'Falha ao sincronizar referências do snapshot '
                '(briefing=%s)', briefing.id,
            )

        if briefing.first_opened_at is None:
            briefing.first_opened_at = timezone.now()
            briefing.save(update_fields=[
                'first_opened_at', 'updated_at',
            ])

        return Response(_serialize(briefing, request))


class PortalBriefingStartView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsPortalSessionAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, briefing_id=None):
        briefing = _resolve_briefing_or_404(briefing_id, request)
        if briefing is None:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Briefing não encontrado', 'code': 'not_found'},
                status=404,
            )

        try:
            BriefingService.mark_intro_seen(briefing)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception(
                'Falha ao marcar intro como vista (briefing=%s)',
                briefing.id,
            )

        briefing.refresh_from_db()
        return Response(_serialize(briefing, request))


class PortalBriefingAnswerView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsPortalSessionAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, briefing_id=None, question_id=None):
        briefing = _resolve_briefing_or_404(briefing_id, request)
        if briefing is None:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Briefing não encontrado', 'code': 'not_found'},
                status=404,
            )

        if briefing.first_opened_at is None:
            briefing.first_opened_at = timezone.now()
            briefing.save(update_fields=[
                'first_opened_at', 'updated_at',
            ])

        try:
            BriefingService.sync_snapshot_references(briefing)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception(
                'Falha ao sincronizar referências antes de responder '
                '(briefing=%s)', briefing.id,
            )

        serializer = PortalAnswerSubmitSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        audio_document = None
        audio_document_id = serializer.validated_data.get('audio_document_id')
        if audio_document_id:
            audio_document = Document.objects.filter(
                id=audio_document_id,
                workspace=briefing.workspace,
            ).first()
            if audio_document is None:
                return Response(
                    {
                        'error': 'Áudio não encontrado',
                        'code': 'audio_not_found',
                    },
                    status=400,
                )
            mime = getattr(audio_document, 'mime_type', '') or ''
            if mime and not mime.startswith('audio/'):
                return Response(
                    {
                        'error': 'O arquivo enviado não é um áudio.',
                        'code': 'not_audio',
                    },
                    status=400,
                )

        attachments = []
        attachment_ids = serializer.validated_data.get('attachment_ids') or []
        if attachment_ids:
            attachments = list(Document.objects.filter(
                id__in=attachment_ids,
                workspace=briefing.workspace,
            ))
            expected = set(str(a) for a in attachment_ids)
            found = set(str(a.id) for a in attachments)
            if expected != found:
                return Response(
                    {
                        'error': 'Uma ou mais imagens não foram encontradas.',
                        'code': 'attachments_not_found',
                    },
                    status=400,
                )
            for doc in attachments:
                mime = getattr(doc, 'mime_type', '') or ''
                if mime and not mime.startswith('image/'):
                    return Response(
                        {
                            'error': 'Um dos arquivos não é uma imagem.',
                            'code': 'not_image',
                        },
                        status=400,
                    )

        try:
            BriefingService.submit_answer(
                briefing_id=briefing.id,
                portal_session=request.portal_session,
                question_id=question_id,
                content_text=serializer.validated_data.get('text', ''),
                audio_document=audio_document,
                attachment_documents=attachments,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = _handle_error(exc, briefing=briefing)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro inesperado ao submeter resposta')
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Erro inesperado.', 'code': 'internal'},
                status=500,
            )

        briefing.refresh_from_db()
        return Response(_serialize(briefing, request))


class PortalBriefingClarificationView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsPortalSessionAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, briefing_id=None, question_id=None):
        briefing = _resolve_briefing_or_404(briefing_id, request)
        if briefing is None:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Briefing não encontrado', 'code': 'not_found'},
                status=404,
            )

        try:
            BriefingService.request_clarification(
                briefing=briefing,
                portal_session=request.portal_session,
                question_id=question_id,
                message=serializer.validated_data['message'],
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = _handle_error(exc, briefing=briefing)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao registrar dúvida')
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Erro inesperado.', 'code': 'internal'},
                status=500,
            )

        briefing.refresh_from_db()
        return Response(_serialize(briefing, request))


class PortalBriefingReviewView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsPortalSessionAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, briefing_id=None, question_id=None):
        briefing = _resolve_briefing_or_404(briefing_id, request)
        if briefing is None:

                portal_session=request.portal_session,
                question_id=question_id,
                message=serializer.validated_data.get('message', ''),
            )
        except Exception as exc:

            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao solicitar revisão')
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Erro inesperado.', 'code': 'internal'},
                status=500,
            )

        briefing.refresh_from_db()
        return Response(_serialize(briefing, request))


class PortalBriefingExportPdfView(APIView):
    """
    GET /api/portal/briefings/<id>/export-pdf/

    Gera o PDF BRUTO do briefing na visão do CLIENTE.
    Devolve a mensagem real de erro se o WeasyPrint falhar.
    """
    permission_classes = [IsPortalSessionAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request, briefing_id=None):
        from io import BytesIO
        from django.http import FileResponse
        from apps.briefings.services.export_service import (
            BriefingExportService,
        )

        briefing = _resolve_briefing_or_404(briefing_id, request)
        if briefing is None:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Briefing não encontrado', 'code': 'not_found'},
                status=404,
            )

        try:
            pdf_bytes, filename = BriefingExportService.render_pdf(
                briefing, request=request, mode='client',
            )
        except RuntimeError as exc:
            logger.warning(
                'Falha ao gerar PDF do cliente (briefing=%s): %s',
                briefing.id, exc,
            )
            return Response(
                {'error': str(exc)},
                status=500,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception(
                'Falha inesperada ao gerar PDF do cliente '
                '(briefing=%s)', briefing.id,
            )
            return Response(
                {'error': f'Erro inesperado: {exc}'},
                status=500,
            )

        return FileResponse(
            BytesIO(pdf_bytes),
            as_attachment=True,
            filename=filename,
            content_type='application/pdf',
        )

"""
BriefingTemplateViewSet — CRUD + actions estruturais.

Capabilities:
    briefings.template.view    → list, retrieve, history,
                                 versions (GET list/detail)
    briefings.template.manage  → create, update, delete, cover,
                                 publish, unpublish, set-published,
                                 archive, sections & questions
                                 CRUD/reorder,
                                 versions (POST/publish/archive/DELETE)

NOTAS SOBRE PATHS:
    - COVER usa paths distintos para POST e DELETE:
          POST   /api/briefing-templates/{id}/cover/
          DELETE /api/briefing-templates/{id}/cover/clear/
    - PUBLISH / UNPUBLISH / SET-PUBLISHED / ARCHIVE:
          POST   /api/briefing-templates/{id}/publish/
          POST   /api/briefing-templates/{id}/unpublish/
          POST   /api/briefing-templates/{id}/set-published/
          POST   /api/briefing-templates/{id}/archive/
    - VERSIONS:
          GET    /api/briefing-templates/{id}/versions/            (list)
          POST   /api/briefing-templates/{id}/versions/            (cria rascunho OU restaura)
          GET    /api/briefing-templates/{id}/versions/{vid}/      (detalhe)
          DELETE /api/briefing-templates/{id}/versions/{vid}/      (exclui)
          POST   /api/briefing-templates/{id}/versions/{vid}/publish/
          POST   /api/briefing-templates/{id}/versions/{vid}/archive/

SOBRE O POST /versions/:
    Body aceita:
        { "mode": "clone_current" }
        { "mode": "blank" }
        { "mode": "restore_from_version", "source_version_id": "<uuid>" }

    `restore_from_version` SUBSTITUI a estrutura atual do modelo
    pela estrutura da versão escolhida. Requer template em `draft`.
"""

import logging

from rest_framework import status, viewsets
from rest_framework.decorators import action
from rest_framework.parsers import (
    MultiPartParser, FormParser, JSONParser,
)
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response

from apps.briefings.models import (
    BriefingSection, BriefingQuestion, BriefingTemplateEvent,
)
from apps.briefings.api.serializers import (
    BriefingTemplateListSerializer,
    BriefingTemplateDetailSerializer,
    BriefingTemplateCreateSerializer,
    BriefingTemplateUpdateSerializer,
    BriefingSectionSerializer,
    BriefingSectionCreateSerializer,
    BriefingSectionUpdateSerializer,
    BriefingQuestionSerializer,
    BriefingQuestionCreateSerializer,
    BriefingQuestionUpdateSerializer,
    BriefingTemplateEventSerializer,
    BriefingTemplateVersionSerializer,
    BriefingTemplateVersionListSerializer,
    BriefingTemplateVersionCreateSerializer,
    ReorderSerializer,
)
from apps.briefings.services import (
    BriefingTemplateService,
    BriefingTemplateVersionService,
)
from apps.briefings.exceptions import (
    BriefingError,
    BriefingTemplateNotFoundError,
    BriefingTemplateNotDraftError,
    BriefingTemplateVersionNotFoundError,
    BriefingSectionNotFoundError,
    BriefingQuestionNotFoundError,
)
from apps.workspaces.queries import WorkspaceQueries
from apps.workspaces.models import WorkspaceMembership
from apps.console.models import ConsoleGovernanceCapability

logger = logging.getLogger('briefings')


class BriefingTemplateViewSet(viewsets.GenericViewSet):

    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    parser_classes = [MultiPartParser, FormParser, JSONParser]
    lookup_field = 'id'

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # CONTEXTO
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _current_workspace(self):
        workspace_id = self.request.session.get('current_workspace_id')
        if not workspace_id:
            return None
        return WorkspaceQueries.get_for_user(workspace_id, self.request.user)

    def _require_capability(self, workspace, capability_code):
        membership = WorkspaceMembership.objects.filter(
            workspace=workspace,
            user=self.request.user,
        ).select_related('governance').first()

        if membership is None:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Você não é membro deste Workspace'},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            )
        if membership.governance.key == 'proprietario':
            return None
        has = ConsoleGovernanceCapability.objects.filter(
            governance=membership.governance,
            capability__code=capability_code,
            capability__is_active=True,
        ).exists()
        if not has:
            return Response(
                {'error': f'Sua governança não permite esta ação ({capability_code}).'},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            )
        return None

    def _get_template_or_404(self, template_id, workspace):
        return BriefingTemplateService.get_or_raise(template_id, workspace)

    @staticmethod
    def _handle_domain_error(exc):
        if isinstance(exc, BriefingTemplateNotFoundError):
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=404)
        if isinstance(exc, BriefingTemplateVersionNotFoundError):
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=404)
        if isinstance(exc, BriefingSectionNotFoundError):
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=404)
        if isinstance(exc, BriefingQuestionNotFoundError):
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=404)
        if isinstance(exc, BriefingTemplateNotDraftError):
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=409)
        if isinstance(exc, BriefingError):
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=400)
        return None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # TEMPLATE — CRUD
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def list(self, request):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.view')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        qs = workspace.briefing_templates.all().order_by('position', '-created_at')

        category = request.query_params.get('category')
        if category:
            qs = qs.filter(category=category)

        category_id = request.query_params.get('category_id')
        if category_id:
            qs = qs.filter(category_fk_id=category_id)

        status_filter = request.query_params.get('status')
        if status_filter:
            qs = qs.filter(status=status_filter)

        return Response(
            BriefingTemplateListSerializer(
                qs, many=True, context={'request': request},
            ).data
        )

    def create(self, request):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        serializer = BriefingTemplateCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            template = BriefingTemplateService.create_template(
                workspace=workspace,
                name=serializer.validated_data['name'],
                description=serializer.validated_data.get('description', ''),
                category=serializer.validated_data.get('category', ''),
                intro_message=serializer.validated_data.get('intro_message', ''),
                actor=request.user,
            )
            category_id = serializer.validated_data.get('category_id')
            if category_id:
                template.category_fk_id = category_id
                template.save(update_fields=['category_fk', 'updated_at'])
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao criar template')
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Erro inesperado ao criar o template.'},
                status=500,
            )

        return Response(
            BriefingTemplateDetailSerializer(
                template, context={'request': request},
            ).data,
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )

    def retrieve(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.view')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingTemplateDetailSerializer(
                template, context={'request': request},
            ).data
        )

    def partial_update(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        serializer = BriefingTemplateUpdateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            template = BriefingTemplateService.update_template(
                template=template,
                name=serializer.validated_data.get('name'),
                description=serializer.validated_data.get('description'),
                category=serializer.validated_data.get('category'),
                intro_message=serializer.validated_data.get('intro_message'),
                actor=request.user,
            )
            if 'category_id' in request.data:
                category_id = serializer.validated_data.get('category_id') or None
                template.category_fk_id = category_id
                template.save(update_fields=['category_fk', 'updated_at'])
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao editar template')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingTemplateDetailSerializer(
                template, context={'request': request},
            ).data
        )

    def destroy(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
            BriefingTemplateService.delete_template(
                template=template, actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao excluir template')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(status=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # REORDER
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='reorder')
    def reorder(self, request):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        serializer = ReorderSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            BriefingTemplateService.reorder_templates(
                workspace=workspace,
                ordered_ids=serializer.validated_data['ordered_ids'],
                actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao reordenar templates')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response({'ok': True})

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # COVER
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @action(
        detail=True,
        methods=['post'],
        url_path='cover',
        url_name='template-cover-set',
        parser_classes=[MultiPartParser, FormParser],
    )
    def set_cover(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        uploaded = request.FILES.get('cover') or request.FILES.get('file')
        if uploaded is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhuma imagem enviada'}, status=400)

        try:
            template = BriefingTemplateService.set_cover(
                template=template,
                uploaded_file=uploaded,
                actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao salvar capa')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingTemplateDetailSerializer(
                template, context={'request': request},
            ).data
        )

    @action(
        detail=True,
        methods=['delete'],
        url_path='cover/clear',
        url_name='template-cover-clear',
    )
    def clear_cover(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
            template = BriefingTemplateService.clear_cover(
                template=template, actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao remover capa')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingTemplateDetailSerializer(
                template, context={'request': request},
            ).data
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # PUBLISH / UNPUBLISH / SET-PUBLISHED / ARCHIVE
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def publish(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()

        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)

            )
        except Exception as exc:

                return handled

            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingTemplateDetailSerializer(
                template, context={'request': request},
            ).data
        )

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='unpublish')
    def unpublish(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
            template = BriefingTemplateService.unpublish_template(
                template=template, actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao despublicar template')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingTemplateDetailSerializer(
                template, context={'request': request},
            ).data
        )

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='set-published')
    def set_published(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        published = request.data.get('published')
        if not isinstance(published, bool):
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Campo "published" deve ser booleano.'},
                status=400,
            )

        try:
            if published:
                template = BriefingTemplateService.publish_template(
                    template=template, actor=request.user,
                )
            else:
                template = BriefingTemplateService.unpublish_template(
                    template=template, actor=request.user,
                )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao alterar estado de publicação')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingTemplateDetailSerializer(
                template, context={'request': request},
            ).data
        )

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='archive')
    def archive(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
            template = BriefingTemplateService.archive_template(
                template=template, actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao arquivar template')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingTemplateDetailSerializer(
                template, context={'request': request},
            ).data
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # HISTORY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @action(detail=True, methods=['get'], url_path='history')
    def history(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.view')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        qs = (
            BriefingTemplateEvent.objects
            .filter(template=template)
            .select_related('user')
            .order_by('-created_at')[:200]
        )
        return Response(BriefingTemplateEventSerializer(qs, many=True).data)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # VERSIONS
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @action(detail=True, methods=['get', 'post'], url_path='versions')
    def versions(self, request, id=None):
        """
        GET  → lista todas as versões do template (metadados apenas,
               SEM `structure`). Consumido pela listagem do painel.
        POST → cria uma nova versão em rascunho OU restaura uma existente.

        Body do POST:
            { "mode": "clone_current" }        # padrão
            { "mode": "blank" }
            { "mode": "restore_from_version",
              "source_version_id": "<uuid>" }
        """
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)

        if request.method == 'GET':
            err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.view')
        else:
            err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        if request.method == 'GET':
            qs = BriefingTemplateVersionService.list_for_template(template)
            return Response(
                BriefingTemplateVersionListSerializer(qs, many=True).data
            )

        # POST — cria nova versão
        serializer = BriefingTemplateVersionCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        mode = serializer.validated_data.get('mode', 'clone_current')

        try:
            if mode == 'restore_from_version':
                source = BriefingTemplateVersionService.get_or_raise(
                    serializer.validated_data['source_version_id'],
                    template,
                )
                version = (
                    BriefingTemplateVersionService.restore_into_template(
                        template=template,
                        source_version=source,
                        actor=request.user,
                    )
                )
            else:
                version = (
                    BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
                        template=template,
                        actor=request.user,
                        blank=(mode == 'blank'),
                    )
                )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao criar/restaurar versão em rascunho')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingTemplateVersionSerializer(version).data,
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )

    @action(
        detail=True,
        methods=['get', 'delete'],
        url_path=r'versions/(?P<version_id>[^/.]+)',
    )
    def version_detail(self, request, id=None, version_id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)

        if request.method == 'GET':
            err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.view')
        else:
            err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        try:
            version = BriefingTemplateVersionService.get_or_raise(
                version_id, template,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        if request.method == 'GET':
            return Response(
                BriefingTemplateVersionSerializer(version).data
            )

        try:
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.delete(
                version=version, actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao excluir versão')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(status=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)

    @action(
        detail=True,
        methods=['post'],
        url_path=r'versions/(?P<version_id>[^/.]+)/publish',
    )
    def publish_version(self, request, id=None, version_id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        try:
            version = BriefingTemplateVersionService.get_or_raise(
                version_id, template,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        try:
            version = BriefingTemplateVersionService.publish(
                version=version, actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao publicar versão')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(BriefingTemplateVersionSerializer(version).data)

    @action(
        detail=True,
        methods=['post'],
        url_path=r'versions/(?P<version_id>[^/.]+)/archive',
    )
    def archive_version(self, request, id=None, version_id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        try:
            version = BriefingTemplateVersionService.get_or_raise(
                version_id, template,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        try:
            version = BriefingTemplateVersionService.archive(
                version=version, actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao arquivar versão')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(BriefingTemplateVersionSerializer(version).data)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # SECTIONS
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='sections')
    def add_section(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        serializer = BriefingSectionCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            section = BriefingTemplateService.add_section(
                template=template,
                title=serializer.validated_data['title'],
                description=serializer.validated_data.get('description', ''),
                actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao adicionar sessão')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingSectionSerializer(section).data,
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )

    @action(
        detail=True, methods=['patch', 'delete'],
        url_path=r'sections/(?P<section_id>[^/.]+)',
    )
    def section_detail(self, request, id=None, section_id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        section = BriefingSection.objects.filter(
            id=section_id, template=template,
        ).first()
        if section is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Sessão não encontrada'}, status=404)

        if request.method == 'DELETE':
            try:
                BriefingTemplateService.delete_section(
                    section=section, actor=request.user,
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
                if handled is not None:
                    return handled
                logger.exception('Erro ao excluir sessão')
                return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)
            return Response(status=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)

        serializer = BriefingSectionUpdateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            section = BriefingTemplateService.update_section(
                section=section,
                title=serializer.validated_data.get('title'),
                description=serializer.validated_data.get('description'),
                actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao editar sessão')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(BriefingSectionSerializer(section).data)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='sections/reorder')
    def reorder_sections(self, request, id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        serializer = ReorderSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            BriefingTemplateService.reorder_sections(
                template=template,
                ordered_ids=serializer.validated_data['ordered_ids'],
                actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao reordenar sessões')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingTemplateDetailSerializer(
                template, context={'request': request},
            ).data
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # QUESTIONS
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @action(
        detail=True, methods=['post'],
        url_path=r'sections/(?P<section_id>[^/.]+)/questions',
    )
    def add_question(self, request, id=None, section_id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        section = BriefingSection.objects.filter(
            id=section_id, template=template,
        ).first()
        if section is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Sessão não encontrada'}, status=404)

        serializer = BriefingQuestionCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            question = BriefingTemplateService.add_question(
                section=section,
                text=serializer.validated_data['text'],
                instructions=serializer.validated_data.get('instructions', ''),
                question_type=serializer.validated_data.get(
                    'question_type', BriefingQuestion.QUESTION_TYPE_TEXT,
                ),
                allowed_response_formats=serializer.validated_data.get(
                    'allowed_response_formats',
                ),
                options=serializer.validated_data.get('options'),
                is_required=serializer.validated_data.get('is_required', True),
                reference_document_ids=serializer.validated_data.get(
                    'reference_document_ids', [],
                ),
                actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao adicionar pergunta')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingQuestionSerializer(
                question, context={'request': request},
            ).data,
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )

    @action(
        detail=True, methods=['patch', 'delete'],
        url_path=r'questions/(?P<question_id>[^/.]+)',
    )
    def question_detail(self, request, id=None, question_id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        question = BriefingQuestion.objects.filter(
            id=question_id, section__template=template,
        ).first()
        if question is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Pergunta não encontrada'}, status=404)

        if request.method == 'DELETE':
            try:
                BriefingTemplateService.delete_question(
                    question=question, actor=request.user,
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
                if handled is not None:
                    return handled
                logger.exception('Erro ao excluir pergunta')
                return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)
            return Response(status=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)

        serializer = BriefingQuestionUpdateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            question = BriefingTemplateService.update_question(
                question=question,
                text=serializer.validated_data.get('text'),
                instructions=serializer.validated_data.get('instructions'),
                question_type=serializer.validated_data.get('question_type'),
                allowed_response_formats=serializer.validated_data.get(
                    'allowed_response_formats',
                ),
                options=serializer.validated_data.get('options'),
                is_required=serializer.validated_data.get('is_required'),
                reference_document_ids=serializer.validated_data.get(
                    'reference_document_ids',
                ),
                actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao editar pergunta')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(
            BriefingQuestionSerializer(
                question, context={'request': request},
            ).data
        )

    @action(
        detail=True, methods=['post'],
        url_path=r'sections/(?P<section_id>[^/.]+)/questions/reorder',
    )
    def reorder_questions(self, request, id=None, section_id=None):
        workspace = self._current_workspace()
        if workspace is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Nenhum workspace ativo'}, status=400)
        err = self._require_capability(workspace, 'briefings.template.manage')
        if err is not None:
            return err

        try:
            template = self._get_template_or_404(id, workspace)
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            return Response({'error': 'Erro'}, status=500)

        section = BriefingSection.objects.filter(
            id=section_id, template=template,
        ).first()
        if section is None:
            return Response({'error': 'Sessão não encontrada'}, status=404)

        serializer = ReorderSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        try:
            BriefingTemplateService.reorder_questions(
                section=section,
                ordered_ids=serializer.validated_data['ordered_ids'],
                actor=request.user,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            handled = self._handle_domain_error(exc)
            if handled is not None:
                return handled
            logger.exception('Erro ao reordenar perguntas')
            return Response({'error': 'Erro inesperado.'}, status=500)

        return Response(BriefingSectionSerializer(section).data)

from django.apps import AppConfig
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _


class BriefingsConfig(AppConfig):
    default_auto_field = 'django.db.models.BigAutoField'
    name = 'apps.briefings'
    verbose_name = _('Briefings')

    def ready(self):
        """
        Registra os signals do módulo.

        SEM este import, os receivers `post_save(BriefingEvent)` e
        `pre_delete(Briefing)` NUNCA são conectados, e a ponte
        `briefings → intelligence` fica inativa em runtime — os
        eventos são criados, mas nunca chegam ao `IngestService`.
        """
        import apps.briefings.signals  # noqa: F401

"""
Exceções do módulo briefings.
"""


class BriefingError(Exception):
    """Exceção base do módulo briefings."""
    pass


# =====================================================================
# TEMPLATE
# =====================================================================

class BriefingTemplateError(BriefingError):
    pass


class BriefingTemplateNotFoundError(BriefingTemplateError):
    pass


class BriefingTemplateNotDraftError(BriefingTemplateError):
    pass


class BriefingTemplateInactiveError(BriefingTemplateError):
    """Template inativo não pode originar novas execuções."""
    pass


class BriefingTemplateVersionNotFoundError(BriefingTemplateError):
    """
    Versão de template não encontrada.

    Subclasse específica para permitir que `_handle_domain_error`
    mapeie para 404 em vez do 400 genérico de `BriefingError`.
    """
    pass


# =====================================================================
# SECTION
# =====================================================================

class BriefingSectionError(BriefingError):
    pass


class BriefingSectionNotFoundError(BriefingSectionError):
    pass


# =====================================================================
# QUESTION
# =====================================================================

class BriefingQuestionError(BriefingError):
    pass


class BriefingQuestionNotFoundError(BriefingQuestionError):
    pass


# =====================================================================
# EXECUÇÃO
# =====================================================================

class BriefingNotFoundError(BriefingError):
    pass


class BriefingAlreadyCompletedError(BriefingError):
    pass


class BriefingClosedError(BriefingError):
    pass


class BriefingExpiredError(BriefingError):
    pass


class BriefingPausedError(BriefingError):
    pass


class InvalidQuestionSequenceError(BriefingError):
    pass


class InvalidResponseFormatError(BriefingError):
    pass


class BriefingAnswerNotFoundError(BriefingError):
    pass


class BriefingReopenError(BriefingError):
    pass


# =====================================================================
# NOTAS
# =====================================================================

class BriefingNoteError(BriefingError):
    pass


class BriefingNoteNotFoundError(BriefingNoteError):
    pass


# =====================================================================
# TRANSCRIÇÃO
# =====================================================================

class TranscriptionError(BriefingError):
    pass

        ),

"""
Modelo BriefingAnswer — projeção do estado atual de uma pergunta.

REGRA ARQUITETURAL
------------------
BriefingAnswer NÃO substitui o histórico. Ele é uma VIEW do estado
atual, mantida em sincronia com os eventos.

Toda pergunta respondida tem UM e apenas UM BriefingAnswer. Novas
respostas atualizam o registro (apontando para o novo evento), mas
o histórico de respostas anteriores permanece em BriefingEvent.

`state` reflete o estado DA PERGUNTA:

    pending               → ainda não respondida
    answered              → respondida e sem pendências
    pending_clarification → cliente sinalizou dúvida
    review_requested      → cliente pediu nova oportunidade de responder

`transcription_status` é INDEPENDENTE do estado da resposta.

PRESERVAÇÃO DO ÁUDIO
--------------------
`latest_audio` usa PROTECT. Um Document que representa um áudio
de resposta de briefing NÃO pode ser excluído enquanto a projeção
existir. Isso garante o requisito "preserve o arquivo de áudio
original". Se for necessário remover o áudio, o caminho é excluir
o Briefing inteiro — DELETE real em cascata.
"""

import uuid

from django.db import models
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _


class BriefingAnswer(models.Model):

    STATE_PENDING = 'pending'
    STATE_ANSWERED = 'answered'
    STATE_PENDING_CLARIFICATION = 'pending_clarification'
    STATE_REVIEW_REQUESTED = 'review_requested'

    STATE_CHOICES = [
        (STATE_PENDING, 'Aguardando resposta'),
        (STATE_ANSWERED, 'Respondida'),
        (STATE_PENDING_CLARIFICATION, 'Dúvida aberta'),
        (STATE_REVIEW_REQUESTED, 'Revisão solicitada'),
    ]

    TRANSCRIPTION_PENDING = 'pending'
    TRANSCRIPTION_PROCESSING = 'processing'
    TRANSCRIPTION_COMPLETED = 'completed'
    TRANSCRIPTION_FAILED = 'failed'
    TRANSCRIPTION_NOT_APPLICABLE = 'not_applicable'

    TRANSCRIPTION_STATUS_CHOICES = [
        (TRANSCRIPTION_PENDING, 'Aguardando processamento'),
        (TRANSCRIPTION_PROCESSING, 'Processando'),
        (TRANSCRIPTION_COMPLETED, 'Concluída'),
        (TRANSCRIPTION_FAILED, 'Falhou'),
        (TRANSCRIPTION_NOT_APPLICABLE, 'Não aplicável'),
    ]

    id = models.UUIDField(
        primary_key=True,
        default=uuid.uuid4,
        editable=False,
    )

    briefing = models.ForeignKey(
        'briefings.Briefing',
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='answers',
    )

    question_id = models.UUIDField(
        db_index=True,
        help_text=_('UUID da pergunta no snapshot da estrutura'),
    )

    state = models.CharField(
        max_length=30,
        choices=STATE_CHOICES,
        default=STATE_PENDING,
        db_index=True,
    )

    latest_content = models.TextField(
        blank=True,
        help_text=_('Texto da resposta mais recente (se aplicável)'),
    )

    latest_audio = models.ForeignKey(
        'documents.Document',
        on_delete=models.PROTECT,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='briefing_answer_audio',
        help_text=_(
            'Áudio original enviado pelo cliente. PROTECT: o Document '
            'não pode ser excluído enquanto esta projeção existir.'
        ),
    )

    latest_transcription = models.TextField(
        blank=True,
        help_text=_('Transcrição do áudio mais recente'),
    )

    transcription_status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=TRANSCRIPTION_STATUS_CHOICES,
        default=TRANSCRIPTION_NOT_APPLICABLE,
        db_index=True,
    )

    latest_event = models.ForeignKey(
        'briefings.BriefingEvent',
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='answers_as_latest',
        help_text=_('Evento que originou o estado atual'),
    )

    attachments = models.ManyToManyField(
        'documents.Document',
        blank=True,
        related_name='briefing_answer_attachments',
        help_text=_('Imagens anexadas a esta resposta'),
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'briefing_answer'
        verbose_name = _('Resposta de Briefing')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Respostas de Briefing')
        ordering = ['created_at']
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=['briefing', 'question_id'],
                name='briefing_answer_unique_per_question',
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['briefing', 'state']),
            models.Index(fields=['briefing', 'transcription_status']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.briefing_id} · {self.question_id} · {self.state}"

"""
Modelo BriefingEvent — histórico imutável da conversa.

REGRA ARQUITETURAL
------------------
Eventos são APPEND-ONLY. Nunca editados, nunca apagados.

Cada linha descreve UM fato ocorrido. O estado atual é derivado
dos eventos mais recentes (via BriefingAnswer), mas o histórico
completo permanece.

AUTORIA
-------
Um evento pode ser criado por:

    - um usuário autenticado do escritório (User)
    - uma sessão autenticada do portal do cliente (ClientPortalSession)
    - o próprio sistema (envio de e-mail, notificações internas)

Exatamente UM dos dois primeiros deve estar preenchido quando o
evento tem origem humana. Eventos de sistema gravam ambos None —
o CheckConstraint permite essa combinação.

NOTAS
-----
Notas (`BriefingNote`) são do escritório, então todo evento
`note.*` carrega `user=<autor>`. Nunca `portal_session`.

NOTA SOBRE ANÁLISE
------------------
Análise de IA NÃO vive aqui. Pertence ao app `intelligence`.
"""

import uuid

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _


class BriefingEvent(models.Model):

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # TIPOS DE EVENTO
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    KIND_ANSWERED = 'answered'
    KIND_CLARIFICATION_REQUESTED = 'clarification_requested'
    KIND_REVIEW_REQUESTED = 'review_requested'
    KIND_TRANSCRIPTION_GENERATED = 'transcription_generated'
    KIND_ANALYSIS_GENERATED = 'analysis_generated'
    KIND_PAUSED = 'paused'
    KIND_RESUMED = 'resumed'
    KIND_REOPENED = 'reopened'
    KIND_COMPLETED = 'completed'
    KIND_CLOSED = 'closed'
    KIND_EXPIRED = 'expired'
    KIND_COMPLETION_EMAIL_SENT = 'completion_email_sent'
    KIND_MANUAL_SEND = 'manual_send'

    # --- Notas (novos) ---
    KIND_NOTE_CREATED = 'note.created'
    KIND_NOTE_UPDATED = 'note.updated'
    KIND_NOTE_DELETED = 'note.deleted'

    KIND_CHOICES = [
        (KIND_ANSWERED, 'Resposta registrada'),
        (KIND_CLARIFICATION_REQUESTED, 'Dúvida solicitada pelo cliente'),
        (KIND_REVIEW_REQUESTED, 'Revisão solicitada pelo cliente'),
        (KIND_TRANSCRIPTION_GENERATED, 'Transcrição gerada'),
        (KIND_ANALYSIS_GENERATED, 'Análise gerada'),
        (KIND_PAUSED, 'Briefing pausado'),
        (KIND_RESUMED, 'Briefing retomado'),
        (KIND_REOPENED, 'Briefing reaberto'),
        (KIND_COMPLETED, 'Briefing concluído'),
        (KIND_CLOSED, 'Briefing encerrado pelo arquiteto'),
        (KIND_EXPIRED, 'Briefing expirado'),
        (KIND_COMPLETION_EMAIL_SENT, 'E-mail de conclusão enviado'),
        (KIND_MANUAL_SEND, 'Briefing enviado manualmente'),
        (KIND_NOTE_CREATED, 'Nota criada'),
        (KIND_NOTE_UPDATED, 'Nota editada'),
        (KIND_NOTE_DELETED, 'Nota excluída'),
    ]

    id = models.UUIDField(
        primary_key=True,
        default=uuid.uuid4,
        editable=False,
    )

    briefing = models.ForeignKey(
        'briefings.Briefing',
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='events',
    )

    kind = models.CharField(
        max_length=40,
        choices=KIND_CHOICES,
        db_index=True,
    )

    question_id = models.UUIDField(
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        db_index=True,
        help_text=_('UUID da pergunta alvo (quando aplicável)'),
    )

    content = models.JSONField(
        default=dict,
        blank=True,
        help_text=_('Payload específico do tipo de evento'),
    )

    user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='briefing_events_as_user',
        help_text=_('Autor do escritório (se aplicável)'),
    )

    portal_session = models.ForeignKey(
        'clients.ClientPortalSession',
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='briefing_events_as_portal',
        help_text=_('Sessão do portal do cliente (se aplicável)'),
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, db_index=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'briefing_event'
        verbose_name = _('Evento de Briefing')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Eventos de Briefing')
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=(
                    models.Q(user__isnull=False, portal_session__isnull=True)
                    | models.Q(user__isnull=True, portal_session__isnull=False)
                    | models.Q(user__isnull=True, portal_session__isnull=True)
                ),
                name='briefing_event_unique_author',
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['briefing', '-created_at']),
            models.Index(fields=['briefing', 'kind', '-created_at']),
            models.Index(fields=['question_id', '-created_at']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        author = (
            self.user.email if self.user
            else (str(self.portal_session_id) if self.portal_session
                  else 'system')
        )
        return f"{self.briefing_id} · {self.kind} · {author}"
