"""
BriefingEventService — porta única de escrita no histórico.

REGRA ARQUITETURAL
------------------
Nenhum ponto do código escreve em `BriefingEvent` diretamente.
Só este service escreve.

Isso garante:
    - consistência do campo `content`
    - autoria explícita (user XOR portal_session, ou ambos None
      para eventos de sistema)
    - eventos de sistema (user=None, portal_session=None) permitidos
      para ações internas (envio de e-mail, notificações), mas
      marcados como tal
"""

from typing import Optional

from apps.briefings.models import Briefing, BriefingEvent
from apps.briefings.exceptions import BriefingError


class BriefingEventService:

    @staticmethod
    def log(
        *,
        briefing: Briefing,
        kind: str,
        user=None,
        portal_session=None,
        question_id=None,
        content: Optional[dict] = None,
    ) -> BriefingEvent:
        """
        Registra um evento no histórico.

        Autoridade:
            - Se `user` preenchido → autor é o usuário do escritório.
            - Se `portal_session` preenchido → autor é o cliente.
            - Se ambos vazios → evento de sistema.
            - Se ambos preenchidos → erro (autor ambíguo).

        Validação:
            - kind precisa estar em KIND_CHOICES
            - user, se passado, precisa estar autenticado
            - portal_session, se passada, precisa estar válida
        """
        if user is not None and portal_session is not None:
            raise BriefingError(
                'Evento não pode ter dois autores '
                '(user e portal_session).'
            )

        valid_kinds = {c[0] for c in BriefingEvent.KIND_CHOICES}
        if kind not in valid_kinds:
            raise BriefingError(f'kind inválido: {kind}')

        if user is not None and not getattr(user, 'is_authenticated', False):
            user = None

        if portal_session is not None and not getattr(
            portal_session, 'is_valid', lambda: True
        )():
            raise BriefingError('Sessão do portal inválida')

        return BriefingEvent.objects.create(
            briefing=briefing,
            kind=kind,
            question_id=question_id,
            content=content or {},
            user=user,
            portal_session=portal_session,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # CONSULTAS
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def list_for_briefing(briefing: Briefing):
        return (
            BriefingEvent.objects
            .filter(briefing=briefing)
            .select_related('user', 'portal_session')
            .order_by('-created_at')
        )

    @staticmethod
    def latest_answer_event(briefing: Briefing, question_id):
        return (
            BriefingEvent.objects
            .filter(
                briefing=briefing,
                question_id=question_id,
                kind=BriefingEvent.KIND_ANSWERED,
            )
            .order_by('-created_at')
            .first()
        )

"""
BriefingExportService — extrai o briefing em PDF.

REGRA ARQUITETURAL
------------------
`briefings` só produz DADO BRUTO, mas em duas visões:

    mode='client'
        Visão do CLIENTE.
        - perguntas + respostas do cliente (texto / áudio / transcrição)
        - imagens escolhidas nas opções
        - imagens enviadas pelo cliente
        - moodboard pessoal (as imagens do cliente juntas)
        - sem referências do arquiteto, sem dados internos.

    mode='architect'
        Visão do ARQUITETO.
        - mesmo dado bruto do cliente
        - MAIS referências do arquiteto (imagens de apoio)
        - MAIS transcrições
        - MAIS audiência administrativa
        - MAIS informações de estado / progresso
        - sem notas privadas (Journal).

TEMPLATES
---------
Os templates de PDF vivem em `ui/templates/portal/`:
    briefing_raw_client_pdf.html
    briefing_raw_architect_pdf.html

Eles são HTML/CSS puro, sem JS. Os templates de navegador
(`briefing_raw_client.html` / `briefing_raw_architect.html`)
continuam independentes e não são usados aqui.

O `intelligence` (futuro) vai consumir esses mesmos dados e produzir
o material final (resumos, análises). `briefings` não faz isso.
"""

import logging
import os
import sys

from django.template.loader import render_to_string
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.briefings.services.briefing_service import BriefingService

logger = logging.getLogger('briefings')


class BriefingExportService:

    CLIENT_TEMPLATE = 'portal/briefing_raw_client_pdf.html'
    ARCHITECT_TEMPLATE = 'portal/briefing_raw_architect_pdf.html'

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Bootstrap do WeasyPrint no Windows
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    _weasyprint_bootstrapped = False

    @classmethod
    def _bootstrap_weasyprint(cls):
        """
        No Windows, o WeasyPrint precisa carregar DLLs do GTK3 via cffi.
        O Python não herda o PATH do sistema em runtime, então registramos
        o diretório das DLLs manualmente (os.add_dll_directory, Python 3.8+)
        antes do primeiro import. É idempotente.
        """
        if cls._weasyprint_bootstrapped:
            return
        cls._weasyprint_bootstrapped = True

        if sys.platform != 'win32':
            return

        candidate_dirs = [
            os.environ.get('WEASYPRINT_DLL_DIRECTORIES', ''),
            r'C:\Program Files\GTK3-Runtime Win64\bin',
            r'C:\Program Files (x86)\GTK3-Runtime Win64\bin',
        ]

        for d in candidate_dirs:
            if d and os.path.isdir(d):
                try:
                    os.add_dll_directory(d)
                    logger.info(
                        'WeasyPrint: DLL directory registrado: %s', d,
                    )
                    break
                except Exception:
                    logger.exception(
                        'WeasyPrint: falha ao registrar DLL dir %s', d,
                    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # API PÚBLICA
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def render_pdf(
        briefing,
        request=None,
        mode: str = 'client',
        template_name: str = None,
    ) -> tuple:
        """
        Retorna (bytes, filename).

        `mode`: 'client' ou 'architect'.
        `template_name`: opcional. Quando informado, sobrepõe o template
        derivado de `mode`. Aceito por compatibilidade com chamadores
        antigos (NotificationService, BriefingSendService).

        Levanta RuntimeError com mensagem CLARA se o WeasyPrint
        não estiver disponível (para o frontend exibir ao usuário).
        """
        if not template_name:
            template_name = (
                BriefingExportService.CLIENT_TEMPLATE
                if mode == 'client'
                else BriefingExportService.ARCHITECT_TEMPLATE
            )

        context = BriefingExportService._build_context(
            briefing, request=request, mode=mode,
        )
        html = render_to_string(
            template_name, context, request=request,
        )

        BriefingExportService._bootstrap_weasyprint()

        try:
            from weasyprint import HTML
        except (ImportError, OSError) as exc:
            raise RuntimeError(
                'WeasyPrint não conseguiu carregar as bibliotecas nativas. '
                'No Windows, instale o GTK3 Runtime e adicione '
                'C:\\Program Files\\GTK3-Runtime Win64\\bin ao PATH, '
                'ou defina WEASYPRINT_DLL_DIRECTORIES apontando para essa '
                f'pasta. Erro original: {exc}'
            ) from exc

        try:
            base_url = (
                request.build_absolute_uri('/') if request else None
            )
            pdf_bytes = HTML(string=html, base_url=base_url).write_pdf()
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception('Falha ao gerar PDF com WeasyPrint')
            raise RuntimeError(
                f'WeasyPrint falhou ao gerar o PDF: {exc}'
            ) from exc

        return pdf_bytes, context['filename']

    @staticmethod
    def _build_context(briefing, request=None, mode='client') -> dict:
        answers_by_qid = {
            str(a.question_id): a
            for a in briefing.answers.select_related(
                'latest_audio', 'latest_event',
            ).prefetch_related('attachments')
        }

        from apps.documents.models import Document

        def _docs_by_id(ids):
            ids = [str(x) for x in (ids or []) if x]
            if not ids:
                return {}
            docs = Document.objects.filter(id__in=ids).only(
                'id', 'name', 'mime_type', 'file',
            )
            return {str(d.id): d for d in docs}

        def _doc_url(doc):
            if not doc or not doc.file:
                return ''
            try:
                url = doc.file.url
            except Exception:
                return ''
            if not url:
                return ''
            if request is not None and not url.startswith(
                ('http://', 'https://'),
            ):
                try:
                    url = request.build_absolute_uri(url)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            return url

        sections_payload = []
        client_images_all = []
        architect_references_all = []

        ordered_sections = sorted(
            briefing.structure_snapshot.get('sections', []),
            key=lambda s: s.get('position', 0),
        )
        for section in ordered_sections:
            questions_payload = []
            ordered_questions = sorted(
                section.get('questions', []),
                key=lambda q: q.get('position', 0),
            )
            for q in ordered_questions:
                qid = str(q['id'])
                answer = answers_by_qid.get(qid)

                reference_images = []
                ref_ids = list(q.get('reference_document_ids') or [])
                if ref_ids:
                    refs_map = _docs_by_id(ref_ids)
                    for rid in ref_ids:
                        d = refs_map.get(str(rid))
                        if not d:
                            continue
                        url = _doc_url(d)
                        if not url:
                            continue
                        entry = {
                            'id': str(d.id),
                            'name': d.name or '',
                            'url': url,
                        }
                        reference_images.append(entry)
                        architect_references_all.append(entry)

                chosen_images = []
                if answer is not None:
                    options = q.get('options') or []
                    content = (answer.latest_content or '').strip()
                    if isinstance(options, list) and content:
                        chosen_texts = {
                            t.strip() for t in content.split(',')
                            if t.strip()
                        }
                        doc_ids = [
                            str(o.get('image_document_id'))
                            for o in options
                            if isinstance(o, dict)
                            and o.get('image_document_id')
                        ]
                        docs_map = _docs_by_id(doc_ids)
                        for o in options:
                            if not isinstance(o, dict):
                                continue
                            opt_text = (o.get('text') or '').strip()
                            if opt_text not in chosen_texts:
                                continue
                            doc_id = o.get('image_document_id')
                            if not doc_id:
                                continue
                            d = docs_map.get(str(doc_id))
                            if not d:
                                continue
                            url = _doc_url(d)
                            if not url:
                                continue
                            entry = {
                                'id': str(d.id),
                                'text': opt_text,
                                'name': d.name or '',
                                'url': url,
                            }
                            chosen_images.append(entry)
                            client_images_all.append(entry)

                attachments = []
                if answer is not None:
                    for doc in answer.attachments.all():
                        url = _doc_url(doc)
                        if not url:
                            continue
                        entry = {
                            'id': str(doc.id),
                            'name': doc.full_name,
                            'url': url,
                        }
                        attachments.append(entry)
                        client_images_all.append(entry)

                latest_content = ''
                latest_transcription = ''
                transcription_status = ''
                audio_url = ''
                updated_at = None
                if answer is not None:
                    latest_content = answer.latest_content or ''
                    latest_transcription = answer.latest_transcription or ''
                    transcription_status = (
                        answer.get_transcription_status_display()
                    )
                    updated_at = answer.updated_at
                    if answer.latest_audio:
                        audio_url = _doc_url(answer.latest_audio)

                questions_payload.append({
                    'id': qid,
                    'text': q.get('text', ''),
                    'instructions': q.get('instructions', ''),
                    'question_type': q.get('question_type', 'text'),
                    'is_required': bool(q.get('is_required', True)),
                    'position': q.get('position', 0),
                    'is_custom': bool(q.get('is_custom', False)),
                    'state': answer.state if answer else 'pending',
                    'latest_content': latest_content,
                    'latest_transcription': latest_transcription,
                    'transcription_status': transcription_status,
                    'audio_url': audio_url,
                    'reference_images': reference_images,
                    'chosen_images': chosen_images,
                    'attachments': attachments,
                    'updated_at': updated_at,
                })
            sections_payload.append({
                'id': section.get('id'),
                'title': section.get('title', ''),
                'description': section.get('description', ''),
                'position': section.get('position', 0),
                'questions': questions_payload,
            })

        progress = BriefingService.compute_progress(briefing)

        now = timezone.now()
        slug = ''.join(
            c if c.isalnum() else '-'
            for c in (briefing.title or 'briefing').lower()
        ).strip('-') or 'briefing'
        suffix = 'cliente' if mode == 'client' else 'arquiteto'
        filename = f"briefing-{slug}-{suffix}-{now:%Y%m%d}.pdf"

        from django.conf import settings
        portal_url = ''
        try:
            base = getattr(settings, 'FRONTEND_URL', '').rstrip('/')
            if not base and request is not None:
                base = request.build_absolute_uri('/').rstrip('/')
            if base:
                portal_url = f'{base}/portal/briefings/{briefing.id}/'
        except Exception:
            portal_url = ''

        return {
            'briefing': briefing,
            'client_name': briefing.client.name if briefing.client else '',
            'workspace_name': (
                briefing.workspace.name if briefing.workspace else ''
            ),
            'portal_url': portal_url,
            'intro_message': briefing.intro_message or '',
            'sections': sections_payload,
            'progress': progress,
            'generated_at': now,
            'filename': filename,
            'mode': mode,
            'client_images_all': client_images_all,
            'architect_references_all': architect_references_all,
        }

"""
BriefingNoteService — Journal do arquiteto.

Notas privadas do escritório. Nunca expostas ao cliente.
Nunca enviadas automaticamente para Intelligence.

Toda mudança emite um `BriefingEvent` (`note.created`,
`note.updated`, `note.deleted`) para que a inteligência acompanhe
a evolução do conhecimento do cliente sobre o briefing.

Esses eventos NUNCA são expostos ao portal. O portal serializer
(`PortalBriefingSerializer`) filtra apenas respostas/perguntas e
não consulta `BriefingNote`.
"""

import logging

from django.db import transaction

from apps.briefings.models import Briefing, BriefingNote, BriefingEvent
from apps.briefings.exceptions import (
    BriefingNoteNotFoundError,
    BriefingError,
)
from apps.briefings.services.event_service import BriefingEventService

logger = logging.getLogger('briefings')


class BriefingNoteService:

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def create_note(
        *,
        briefing: Briefing,
        author,
        content: str,
        question_id=None,
    ) -> BriefingNote:
        content = (content or '').strip()
        if not content:
            raise BriefingError('A anotação não pode ser vazia.')

        if question_id is not None:
            snap = briefing.snapshot_question_by_id(question_id)
            if snap is None:
                raise BriefingError(
                    'Pergunta não encontrada no snapshot deste briefing.'
                )

        note = BriefingNote.objects.create(
            briefing=briefing,
            question_id=question_id,
            author=author,
            content=content,
        )

        BriefingEventService.log(
            briefing=briefing,
            kind=BriefingEvent.KIND_NOTE_CREATED,
            user=author,
            question_id=question_id,
            content={
                'note_id': str(note.id),
                'content_length': len(content),
            },
        )
        return note

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def update_note(
        *, note: BriefingNote, author, content: str,
    ) -> BriefingNote:
        content = (content or '').strip()
        if not content:
            raise BriefingError('A anotação não pode ser vazia.')

        previous_length = len(note.content or '')
        note.content = content
        note.author = author
        note.save(update_fields=['content', 'author', 'updated_at'])

        BriefingEventService.log(
            briefing=note.briefing,
            kind=BriefingEvent.KIND_NOTE_UPDATED,
            user=author,
            question_id=note.question_id,
            content={
                'note_id': str(note.id),
                'previous_content_length': previous_length,
                'new_content_length': len(content),
            },
        )
        return note

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def delete_note(*, note: BriefingNote, actor=None) -> None:
        briefing = note.briefing
        note_id = note.id
        question_id = note.question_id
        author = note.author

        note.delete()

        BriefingEventService.log(
            briefing=briefing,
            kind=BriefingEvent.KIND_NOTE_DELETED,
            user=(
                actor if (actor and getattr(actor, 'is_authenticated', False))
                else author
            ),
            question_id=question_id,
            content={'note_id': str(note_id)},
        )

    @staticmethod
    def get_or_raise(note_id, briefing) -> BriefingNote:
        try:
            note = BriefingNote.objects.filter(
                id=note_id, briefing=briefing,
            ).first()
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            note = None
        if note is None:
            raise BriefingNoteNotFoundError('Anotação não encontrada.')
        return note

    @staticmethod
    def list_for_briefing(briefing: Briefing):
        return (
            BriefingNote.objects
            .filter(briefing=briefing)
            .select_related('author')
            .order_by('-created_at')
        )

    @staticmethod
    def list_for_question(briefing: Briefing, question_id):
        return (
            BriefingNote.objects
            .filter(briefing=briefing, question_id=question_id)
            .select_related('author')
            .order_by('-created_at')
        )

"""
NotificationService — envio automático de e-mail ao concluir briefing.

REGRA ARQUITETURAL
------------------
Duas garantias:

1. IDEMPOTÊNCIA: o envio de conclusão é disparado uma e apenas
   uma vez por briefing. Um `BriefingEvent` com
   `kind='completion_email_sent'` marca que o envio já ocorreu.
   Se a função rodar de novo, ela verifica e sai.

2. NÃO BLOQUEIA: o envio é agendado via `transaction.on_commit`,
   nunca dentro da transação de conclusão do briefing. Se o
   provedor de e-mail estiver fora, o briefing continua concluído.

Falha de envio NÃO grava o evento de idempotência. Isso permite
retentativa (manual ou por cron futuro).
"""

import logging

from apps.briefings.models import Briefing, BriefingEvent
from apps.briefings.services.event_service import BriefingEventService

logger = logging.getLogger('briefings')


class NotificationService:

    KIND_MARKER = BriefingEvent.KIND_COMPLETION_EMAIL_SENT

    # ==================================================================
    # API
    # ==================================================================

    @staticmethod
    def on_briefing_completed(briefing_id) -> bool:
        """
        Chamado quando um briefing vira `completed`.

        Retorna `True` se um e-mail foi disparado nesta chamada,
        `False` se já havia sido disparado antes ou se a situação
        não permite (ex.: briefing não existe).

        Nunca levanta.
        """
        try:
            briefing = Briefing.objects.select_related(
                'client', 'workspace', 'template', 'created_by',
            ).get(id=briefing_id)
        except Briefing.DoesNotExist:
            logger.warning(
                'Briefing %s não existe em on_briefing_completed',
                briefing_id,
            )
            return False

        if briefing.status != Briefing.STATUS_COMPLETED:
            logger.info(
                'Briefing %s não está completed, ignorando notificação',
                briefing_id,
            )
            return False

        if NotificationService._already_notified(briefing):
            logger.info(
                'Briefing %s já teve e-mail de conclusão enviado, '
                'ignorando', briefing_id,
            )
            return False

        try:
            NotificationService._send(briefing)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception(
                'Falha ao enviar e-mail de conclusão '
                '(briefing=%s). Evento de idempotência NÃO foi '
                'gravado — retentativa é possível.',
                briefing_id,
            )
            return False

        # Só marca como enviado após sucesso
        NotificationService._mark_notified(briefing)
        logger.info(
            'E-mail de conclusão enviado (briefing=%s)', briefing_id,
        )
        return True

    # ==================================================================
    # INTERNO
    # ==================================================================

    @staticmethod
    def _already_notified(briefing: Briefing) -> bool:
        return BriefingEvent.objects.filter(
            briefing=briefing,
            kind=NotificationService.KIND_MARKER,
        ).exists()

    @staticmethod
    def _mark_notified(briefing: Briefing) -> None:
        BriefingEventService.log(
            briefing=briefing,
            kind=NotificationService.KIND_MARKER,
            user=None,
            portal_session=None,
            content={
                'architect_email': briefing.created_by.email
                    if briefing.created_by else None,
                'client_email': briefing.client.email or None,
            },
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _send(briefing: Briefing) -> None:
        from apps.accounts.services.email import EmailService

        architect_email = (
            briefing.created_by.email
            if briefing.created_by else None
        )

        # Gera PDF do cliente (sem notas internas).
        # O `render_pdf` usa o template default `portal/briefing_raw_client_pdf.html`.
        client_pdf_bytes = None
        client_pdf_filename = None
        try:
            from apps.briefings.services.export_service import (
                BriefingExportService,
            )
            client_pdf_bytes, client_pdf_filename = (
                BriefingExportService.render_pdf(
                    briefing,
                    mode='client',
                )
            )
        except Exception:
            logger.exception(
                'Falha ao gerar PDF do cliente para envio de '
                'conclusão (briefing=%s). Vai enviar sem anexo.',
                briefing.id,
            )

        client_portal_url = NotificationService._portal_url(briefing)

        if architect_email:
            EmailService.send_briefing_completed(
                briefing=briefing,
                client_portal_url=client_portal_url,
                architect_email=architect_email,
                client_pdf_bytes=client_pdf_bytes,
                client_pdf_filename=client_pdf_filename,
            )
        else:
            # Sem e-mail do arquiteto: ainda envia para o cliente
            if briefing.client.email:
                from django.conf import settings
                site_name = getattr(settings, 'SITE_NAME', 'Clivo')
                EmailService.send(
                    to=briefing.client.email,
                    subject=(
                        f'Seu briefing foi registrado — {briefing.title}'
                    ),
                    text=(
                        f'Olá,\n\n'
                        f'Recebemos suas respostas do briefing '
                        f'"{briefing.title}".\n\n'
                        f'— {site_name}'
                    ),
                )

    @staticmethod
    def _portal_url(briefing: Briefing) -> str:
        """
        Link autenticado do portal do cliente para este briefing.
        """
        from django.conf import settings
        frontend_url = getattr(
            settings, 'FRONTEND_URL', ''
        ).rstrip('/')
        if not frontend_url:
            return ''
        return f'{frontend_url}/'

================================================================================

"""
BriefingQuestionInstanceService — CRUD de perguntas exclusivas.

REGRA ARQUITETURAL
------------------
Perguntas exclusivas só podem ser adicionadas/removidas enquanto o
briefing NÃO tem nenhuma resposta (`is_snapshot_locked()` retorna
False).

Quando a primeira resposta chega, o snapshot do briefing já
incorporou as custom questions — e a partir daí qualquer alteração
invalidaria a estrutura que o cliente está vendo.
"""

import logging

from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.briefings.models import (
    Briefing,
    BriefingQuestionInstance,
)
from apps.briefings.exceptions import (
    BriefingError,
    BriefingNotFoundError,
)

logger = logging.getLogger('briefings')


class BriefingQuestionInstanceService:

    @staticmethod
    def _assert_briefing_editable(briefing: Briefing) -> None:
        """
        Levanta BriefingError se o briefing não aceita mais edições
        estruturais.
        """
        if briefing.status == Briefing.STATUS_COMPLETED:
            raise BriefingError(
                'Este briefing já foi concluído. Não é possível '
                'adicionar ou remover perguntas.'
            )
        if briefing.status == Briefing.STATUS_CLOSED:
            raise BriefingError(
                'Este briefing foi encerrado. Não é possível '
                'adicionar ou remover perguntas.'
            )
        if briefing.status == Briefing.STATUS_EXPIRED:
            raise BriefingError(
                'Este briefing expirou. Não é possível '
                'adicionar ou remover perguntas.'
            )
        if briefing.is_snapshot_locked():
            raise BriefingError(
                'O cliente já começou a responder. Não é possível '
                'alterar a estrutura agora.'
            )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # LISTAGEM
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def list_for_briefing(briefing: Briefing):
        return (
            BriefingQuestionInstance.objects
            .filter(briefing=briefing)
            .order_by('section_ref', 'position', 'created_at')
        )

    @staticmethod
    def list_for_section(briefing: Briefing, section_ref: str):
        return (
            BriefingQuestionInstance.objects
            .filter(briefing=briefing, section_ref=str(section_ref))
            .order_by('position', 'created_at')
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # CRIAÇÃO
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def add_custom_question(
        *,
        briefing: Briefing,
        section_ref: str,
        text: str,
        instructions: str = '',
        question_type: str = 'text',
        allowed_response_formats=None,
        options=None,
        is_required: bool = True,
        actor=None,
    ) -> BriefingQuestionInstance:
        BriefingQuestionInstanceService._assert_briefing_editable(briefing)

        text = (text or '').strip()
        if not text:
            raise BriefingError('O enunciado é obrigatório.')

        section_ref = str(section_ref or '').strip()
        if not section_ref:
            raise BriefingError('A seção é obrigatória.')

        # Confirma que a seção existe no snapshot do briefing
        snapshot = briefing.structure_snapshot or {}
        section_ids = {
            str(s.get('id')) for s in snapshot.get('sections', [])
        }
        if section_ref not in section_ids:
            raise BriefingError(
                'A seção informada não pertence a este briefing.'
            )

        # Próxima posição (respeitando o que já existe no snapshot
        # daquela seção E as custom questions já adicionadas)
        last_position = BriefingQuestionInstanceService._next_position(
            briefing=briefing,
            section_ref=section_ref,
        )

        instance = BriefingQuestionInstance.objects.create(
            briefing=briefing,
            section_ref=section_ref,
            text=text,
            instructions=(instructions or '').strip(),
            question_type=question_type or 'text',
            allowed_response_formats=(
                list(allowed_response_formats)
                if allowed_response_formats is not None
                else ['text', 'audio', 'image']
            ),
            options=list(options) if options else [],
            position=last_position,
            is_required=bool(is_required),
            created_by=actor,
        )

        # Marca o briefing como personalizado
        if not briefing.has_custom_questions:
            briefing.has_custom_questions = True
            briefing.save(update_fields=['has_custom_questions', 'updated_at'])

        return instance

    @staticmethod
    def _next_position(*, briefing: Briefing, section_ref: str) -> int:
        """
        Retorna a próxima posição livre na seção, considerando tanto
        as perguntas do snapshot quanto as custom questions já criadas.
        """
        snapshot_positions = []
        for s in (briefing.structure_snapshot or {}).get('sections', []):
            if str(s.get('id')) != section_ref:
                continue
            for q in s.get('questions', []):
                snapshot_positions.append(int(q.get('position') or 0))

        custom_positions = list(
            BriefingQuestionInstance.objects
            .filter(briefing=briefing, section_ref=section_ref)
            .values_list('position', flat=True)
        )

        all_positions = snapshot_positions + custom_positions
        return (max(all_positions) if all_positions else 0) + 1

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # ATUALIZAÇÃO
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def update_custom_question(
        *,
        instance: BriefingQuestionInstance,
        text: str = None,
        instructions: str = None,
        question_type: str = None,
        allowed_response_formats=None,
        options=None,
        is_required: bool = None,
        actor=None,
    ) -> BriefingQuestionInstance:
        BriefingQuestionInstanceService._assert_briefing_editable(
            instance.briefing,
        )

        fields = []

        if text is not None:
            text = text.strip()
            if not text:
                raise BriefingError('O enunciado não pode ser vazio.')
            instance.text = text
            fields.append('text')

        if instructions is not None:
            instance.instructions = instructions.strip()
            fields.append('instructions')

        if question_type is not None:
            instance.question_type = question_type
            fields.append('question_type')

        if allowed_response_formats is not None:
            instance.allowed_response_formats = list(allowed_response_formats)
            fields.append('allowed_response_formats')

        if options is not None:
            instance.options = list(options)
            fields.append('options')

        if is_required is not None:
            instance.is_required = bool(is_required)
            fields.append('is_required')

        if fields:
            instance.save(update_fields=fields + ['updated_at'])

        return instance

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # EXCLUSÃO
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def remove_custom_question(
        *,
        instance: BriefingQuestionInstance,
        actor=None,
    ) -> None:
        BriefingQuestionInstanceService._assert_briefing_editable(
            instance.briefing,
        )
        instance.delete()

        # Se não sobrou nenhuma, limpa a flag
        remaining = BriefingQuestionInstance.objects.filter(
            briefing=instance.briefing,
        ).exists()
        if not remaining:
            b = instance.briefing
            b.has_custom_questions = False
            b.save(update_fields=['has_custom_questions', 'updated_at'])

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # HELPERS
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def get_or_raise(instance_id, briefing: Briefing):
        try:
            instance = BriefingQuestionInstance.objects.filter(
                id=instance_id, briefing=briefing,
            ).first()
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            instance = None
        if instance is None:
            raise BriefingError('Pergunta exclusiva não encontrada.')
        return instance

"""
SendService — envio manual do briefing pelo arquiteto.

Diferente do NotificationService (automático, apenas em conclusão),
este é acionado pelo arquiteto a qualquer momento:

    - briefing concluído ou não
    - reenvio após ajuste
    - envio para e-mail alternativo

Registra SEMPRE um BriefingEvent `manual_send` com quem enviou,
para quem e quando. Isso torna o reenvio auditável.

REGRA ARQUITETURAL
------------------
- Não gera PDF em modo arquiteto. Sempre em modo cliente,
  sem notas internas.
- Se o envio falhar, o EmailService levanta e o evento NÃO é
  gravado. Isso evita estado inconsistente (marcar como enviado
  um e-mail que nunca saiu).
- Não bloqueia a conclusão do briefing. É uma ação explícita do
  arquiteto, disparada por ele.
"""

import logging

from apps.briefings.models import Briefing
from apps.briefings.services.event_service import BriefingEventService

logger = logging.getLogger('briefings')


class BriefingSendService:

    KIND_MANUAL_SEND = 'manual_send'

    @staticmethod
    def send_to_client(
        *,
        briefing: Briefing,
        actor,
        to_email: str = None,
        message: str = '',
        request=None,
    ) -> dict:
        """
        Envia (ou reenvia) o briefing para o cliente.

        Args:
            briefing: execução
            actor: usuário que está enviando (arquiteto/equipe)
            to_email: destinatário. Se None, usa `briefing.client.email`.
            message: texto opcional a ser incluído no corpo
            request: opcional, para correlation_id e detecção de host

        Returns:
            {
                'sent': bool,
                'to': str,
                'event_id': str,
            }

        Levanta BriefingError se não houver e-mail de destino.

        Levanta outras exceções propagadas do EmailService quando o
        envio falhar (smtp down, credencial inválida, etc.).
        """
        from apps.accounts.services.email import EmailService
        from apps.briefings.services.export_service import (
            BriefingExportService,
        )
        from apps.briefings.exceptions import BriefingError

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 1. Resolve o destinatário
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        target_email = (to_email or '').strip() or (
            briefing.client.email or ''
        )
        if not target_email:
            raise BriefingError(
                'Nenhum e-mail de destino. '
                'Informe um endereço ou cadastre um no cliente.'
            )

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 2. Gera PDF em modo cliente (sem notas internas)
        #
        # Falha na geração NÃO bloqueia o envio. O e-mail ainda é
        # enviado — apenas sem anexo. O evento registra has_pdf=False.
        #
        # O `render_pdf` usa o template default
        # `portal/briefing_raw_client_pdf.html`.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        pdf_bytes = None
        pdf_filename = None
        try:
            pdf_bytes, pdf_filename = BriefingExportService.render_pdf(
                briefing,
                mode='client',
            )
        except Exception:
            logger.exception(
                'Falha ao gerar PDF para envio manual (briefing=%s). '
                'O e-mail será enviado sem anexo.',
                briefing.id,
            )

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 3. Resolve URL do portal do cliente
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        portal_url = ''
        try:
            from django.conf import settings
            base = getattr(settings, 'FRONTEND_URL', '').rstrip('/')
            if base:
                portal_url = f'{base}/'
        except Exception:
            pass

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 4. Envia o e-mail
        #
        # Se o envio falhar, a exceção sobe. O evento NÃO é gravado.
        # Isso evita marcar como enviado algo que não saiu.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        EmailService.send_briefing_to_client(
            briefing=briefing,
            to_email=target_email,
            pdf_bytes=pdf_bytes,
            pdf_filename=pdf_filename,
            message=message,
            client_portal_url=portal_url,
        )

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 5. Registra o evento (append-only) — só após sucesso do envio
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        event = BriefingEventService.log(
            briefing=briefing,
            kind=BriefingSendService.KIND_MANUAL_SEND,
            user=actor,
            content={
                'to': target_email,
                'by': actor.email if actor else None,
                'has_pdf': pdf_bytes is not None,
                'message': message or None,
            },
        )

        logger.info(
            'Briefing %s enviado manualmente para %s por %s',
            briefing.id,
            target_email,
            actor.email if actor else '?',
        )

        return {
            'sent': True,
            'to': target_email,
            'event_id': str(event.id),
        }

"""
Tasks Celery do módulo briefings.

Se Celery não estiver configurado, o fallback inline é usado em
TranscriptionService.run_async.
"""

import logging

logger = logging.getLogger('briefings')


try:
    from celery import shared_task
    _has_celery = True
except ImportError:
    _has_celery = False
    shared_task = None


if _has_celery:

    @shared_task(name='briefings.transcribe_answer', bind=True, max_retries=2)
    def transcribe_answer_task(self, answer_id: str):
        from apps.briefings.services.transcription_service import (
            TranscriptionService,
        )
        try:
            TranscriptionService.transcribe_answer(answer_id)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception('Falha na task de transcrição')
            raise self.retry(exc=exc, countdown=60)

else:
    def transcribe_answer_task(*args, **kwargs):  # pragma: no cover
        raise RuntimeError('Celery não instalado.')

"""
BriefingTemplateService — ciclo de vida do template reutilizável.

Emite `BriefingTemplateEvent` para toda alteração estrutural.

REGRA P0:
    - `delete_template` NÃO apaga execuções em cascata.
    - Versões (`BriefingTemplateVersion`) sobrevivem com
      `template=NULL` quando o modelo lógico é excluído.
    - O snapshot do briefing já contém tudo que precisa para
      continuar funcionando.

PUBLICAÇÃO / DESPUBLICAÇÃO:
    - `publish_template` valida que existem sessões, cria uma nova
      `BriefingTemplateVersion` (rascunho), popula `structure` a partir
      das tabelas atuais, e publica essa versão.
    - `unpublish_template` volta o status do template para `draft`
      SEM tocar em versões publicadas.
    - `archive_template` move o template para `archived`.

Briefings em execução NUNCA são afetados por essas transições —
usam `structure_snapshot` imutável.
"""

import logging

from django.db import transaction

from apps.briefings.models import (
    BriefingTemplate,
    BriefingSection,
    BriefingQuestion,
    BriefingTemplateEvent,
    BriefingTemplateVersion,
)
from apps.briefings.exceptions import (
    BriefingTemplateNotFoundError,
    BriefingTemplateNotDraftError,
    BriefingSectionNotFoundError,
    BriefingQuestionNotFoundError,
    BriefingError,
)

logger = logging.getLogger('briefings')


_REORDER_OFFSET = 100000


class BriefingTemplateService:

    # ==================================================================
    # EVENTOS
    # ==================================================================

    @staticmethod
    def _log_event(
        *, template, kind, user=None,
        section_id=None, question_id=None, content=None,
    ):
        BriefingTemplateEvent.objects.create(
            template=template,
            kind=kind,
            section_id=section_id,
            question_id=question_id,
            content=content or {},
            user=user if (user and getattr(user, 'is_authenticated', False)) else None,
        )

    # ==================================================================
    # TEMPLATE
    # ==================================================================

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def create_template(
        *, workspace, name: str, description: str = '',
        category: str = '', intro_message: str = '', actor=None,
    ) -> BriefingTemplate:
        name = (name or '').strip()
        if not name:
            raise BriefingError('O nome do template é obrigatório.')

        last = (
            BriefingTemplate.objects
            .filter(workspace=workspace)
            .order_by('-position')
            .first()
        )

        template = BriefingTemplate.objects.create(
            workspace=workspace,
            name=name,
            description=(description or '').strip(),
            category=(category or '').strip(),
            intro_message=(intro_message or '').strip(),
            status=BriefingTemplate.STATUS_DRAFT,
            position=(last.position + 1 if last else 1),
            created_by=actor,
        )

        BriefingTemplateVersion.objects.create(
            template=template,
            version_number=1,
            status=BriefingTemplateVersion.STATUS_DRAFT,
            structure={},
            created_by=actor,
        )

        BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
            template=template,
            kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_TEMPLATE_CREATED,
            user=actor,
            content={'name': template.name},
        )
        return template

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def update_template(
        *, template: BriefingTemplate,
        name: str = None, description: str = None, category: str = None,
        intro_message: str = None, actor=None,
    ) -> BriefingTemplate:
        fields = []
        changes = {}

        if name is not None:
            name = name.strip()
            if not name:
                raise BriefingError('O nome do template não pode ser vazio.')
            if template.name != name:
                changes['name'] = {'old': template.name, 'new': name}
                template.name = name
                fields.append('name')

        if description is not None:
            new_desc = description.strip()
            if template.description != new_desc:
                changes['description'] = True
                template.description = new_desc
                fields.append('description')

        if category is not None:
            new_cat = category.strip()
            if template.category != new_cat:
                changes['category'] = {
                    'old': template.category, 'new': new_cat,
                }
                template.category = new_cat
                fields.append('category')

        if intro_message is not None:
            new_intro = (intro_message or '').strip()
            if template.intro_message != new_intro:
                changes['intro_message'] = True
                template.intro_message = new_intro
                fields.append('intro_message')

        if fields:
            template.save(update_fields=fields + ['updated_at'])
            BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
                template=template,
                kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_TEMPLATE_UPDATED,
                user=actor,
                content={'changes': changes},
            )
        return template

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def set_cover(*, template: BriefingTemplate, uploaded_file, actor=None):
        if uploaded_file is None:
            raise BriefingError('Nenhuma imagem enviada.')

        name = getattr(uploaded_file, 'name', '') or ''
        ext = name.rsplit('.', 1)[-1].lower() if '.' in name else ''
        if ext not in {'jpg', 'jpeg', 'jfif', 'png', 'webp', 'gif'}:
            raise BriefingError(
                'Formato inválido. Envie JPG, PNG, WEBP ou GIF.'
            )

        old_name = None
        old_storage = None
        if template.cover:
            try:
                old_name = template.cover.name
                old_storage = template.cover.storage
            except Exception:
                pass

        try:
            template.cover.save(name, uploaded_file, save=False)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception('Falha ao salvar capa do template')
            raise BriefingError('Falha ao salvar a capa no storage.') from exc

        template.save(update_fields=['cover', 'updated_at'])

        if old_name and old_storage:
            try:
                old_storage.delete(old_name)
            except Exception:
                logger.warning(
                    'Não foi possível remover a capa antiga: %s', old_name,
                )

        BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
            template=template,
            kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_TEMPLATE_UPDATED,
            user=actor,
            content={'action': 'cover_changed'},
        )
        return template

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def clear_cover(*, template: BriefingTemplate, actor=None):
        if not template.cover:
            return template
        old_name = template.cover.name
        old_storage = template.cover.storage
        template.cover = None
        template.save(update_fields=['cover', 'updated_at'])
        try:
            old_storage.delete(old_name)
        except Exception:
            logger.warning('Não foi possível remover a capa: %s', old_name)
        BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
            template=template,
            kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_TEMPLATE_UPDATED,
            user=actor,
            content={'action': 'cover_removed'},
        )
        return template

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def delete_template(*, template: BriefingTemplate, actor=None) -> None:
        BriefingTemplateVersion.objects.filter(template=template).update(
            template=None,
        )

        if template.cover:
            try:
                template.cover.delete(save=False)
            except Exception:
                logger.warning('Falha ao remover capa ao excluir template')

        template.delete()

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def publish_template(
        *, template: BriefingTemplate, actor=None,
    ) -> BriefingTemplate:
        if template.status != BriefingTemplate.STATUS_DRAFT:
            raise BriefingTemplateNotDraftError(
                'Só templates em rascunho podem ser publicados.'
            )
        if not template.sections.exists():
            raise BriefingError('Não é possível publicar sem sessões.')

        from apps.briefings.services.template_version_service import (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService,
        )

        version = BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
            template=template, actor=actor,
        )
        BriefingTemplateVersionService.publish(
            version=version, actor=actor,
        )

        template.status = BriefingTemplate.STATUS_PUBLISHED
        template.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])

        BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
            template=template,
            kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_TEMPLATE_PUBLISHED,
            user=actor,
            content={
                'version_id': str(version.id),
                'version_number': version.version_number,
            },
        )
        return template

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def unpublish_template(
        *, template: BriefingTemplate, actor=None,
    ) -> BriefingTemplate:
        if template.status != BriefingTemplate.STATUS_PUBLISHED:
            raise BriefingError(
                'Só templates publicados podem voltar para rascunho.'
            )

        template.status = BriefingTemplate.STATUS_DRAFT
        template.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])

        BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
            template=template,
            kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_TEMPLATE_UNPUBLISHED,
            user=actor,
        )
        return template

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def archive_template(
        *, template: BriefingTemplate, actor=None,
    ) -> BriefingTemplate:
        if template.status == BriefingTemplate.STATUS_ARCHIVED:
            return template

        template.status = BriefingTemplate.STATUS_ARCHIVED
        template.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])

        BriefingTemplateVersion.objects.filter(
            template=template,
            status=BriefingTemplateVersion.STATUS_PUBLISHED,
        ).update(
            status=BriefingTemplateVersion.STATUS_ARCHIVED,
        )

        BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
            template=template,
            kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_TEMPLATE_ARCHIVED,
            user=actor,
        )
        return template

    @staticmethod
    def get_or_raise(template_id, workspace) -> BriefingTemplate:
        try:
            template = (
                BriefingTemplate.objects
                .filter(id=template_id, workspace=workspace)
                .first()
            )
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            template = None
        if template is None:
            raise BriefingTemplateNotFoundError('Template não encontrado.')
        return template

    # ==================================================================
    # REORDER — TEMPLATES
    # ==================================================================

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def reorder_templates(
        *, workspace, ordered_ids: list, actor=None,
    ) -> None:
        templates = {
            str(t.id): t
            for t in BriefingTemplate.objects.filter(workspace=workspace)
        }

        for tid in ordered_ids:
            if str(tid) not in templates:
                raise BriefingTemplateNotFoundError(
                    f'Modelo {tid} não pertence ao workspace.'
                )

        for i, tid in enumerate(ordered_ids):
            t = templates[str(tid)]
            t.position = _REORDER_OFFSET + i
            t.save(update_fields=['position'])

        for i, tid in enumerate(ordered_ids):
            t = templates[str(tid)]
            t.position = i + 1
            t.save(update_fields=['position'])

    # ==================================================================
    # SECTION
    # ==================================================================

    @staticmethod
    def _next_section_position(template: BriefingTemplate) -> int:
        last = (
            BriefingSection.objects
            .filter(template=template)
            .order_by('-position')
            .values_list('position', flat=True)
            .first()
        )
        return (last or 0) + 1

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def add_section(
        *, template: BriefingTemplate, title: str,
        description: str = '', actor=None,
    ) -> BriefingSection:
        if not template.is_editable():
            raise BriefingTemplateNotDraftError(
                'Só templates em rascunho podem ser editados.'
            )
        title = (title or '').strip()
        if not title:
            raise BriefingError('O título da sessão é obrigatório.')

        section = BriefingSection.objects.create(
            template=template,
            title=title,
            description=(description or '').strip(),
            position=BriefingTemplateService._next_section_position(template),
        )
        BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
            template=template,
            kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_SECTION_CREATED,
            user=actor,
            section_id=section.id,
            content={'title': section.title, 'position': section.position},
        )
        return section

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def update_section(
        *, section: BriefingSection,
        title: str = None, description: str = None, actor=None,
    ) -> BriefingSection:
        if not section.template.is_editable():
            raise BriefingTemplateNotDraftError(
                'Só templates em rascunho podem ser editados.'
            )
        fields = []
        changes = {}
        if title is not None:
            title = title.strip()
            if not title:
                raise BriefingError('O título não pode ser vazio.')
            if section.title != title:
                changes['title'] = {'old': section.title, 'new': title}
                section.title = title
                fields.append('title')
        if description is not None:
            new_desc = description.strip()
            if section.description != new_desc:
                changes['description'] = True
                section.description = new_desc
                fields.append('description')
        if fields:
            section.save(update_fields=fields + ['updated_at'])
            BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
                template=section.template,
                kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_SECTION_UPDATED,
                user=actor,
                section_id=section.id,
                content={'changes': changes},
            )
        return section

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def delete_section(*, section: BriefingSection, actor=None) -> None:
        if not section.template.is_editable():
            raise BriefingTemplateNotDraftError(
                'Só templates em rascunho podem ser editados.'
            )
        template = section.template
        section_id = section.id
        title = section.title
        section.delete()
        BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
            template=template,
            kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_SECTION_DELETED,
            user=actor,
            section_id=section_id,
            content={'title': title},
        )

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def reorder_sections(
        *, template: BriefingTemplate, ordered_ids: list, actor=None,
    ) -> None:
        if not template.is_editable():
            raise BriefingTemplateNotDraftError(
                'Só templates em rascunho podem ser editados.'
            )

        sections = {
            str(s.id): s
            for s in BriefingSection.objects.filter(template=template)
        }
        for sid in ordered_ids:
            if str(sid) not in sections:
                raise BriefingSectionNotFoundError(
                    f'Sessão {sid} não pertence ao template.'
                )

        for i, sid in enumerate(ordered_ids):
            s = sections[str(sid)]
            s.position = _REORDER_OFFSET + i
            s.save(update_fields=['position'])

        for i, sid in enumerate(ordered_ids):
            s = sections[str(sid)]
            s.position = i + 1
            s.save(update_fields=['position'])

        BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
            template=template,
            kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_SECTION_REORDERED,
            user=actor,
            content={'ordered_ids': [str(x) for x in ordered_ids]},
        )

    # ==================================================================
    # QUESTION
    # ==================================================================

    @staticmethod
    def _next_question_position(section: BriefingSection) -> int:
        last = (
            BriefingQuestion.objects
            .filter(section=section)
            .order_by('-position')
            .values_list('position', flat=True)
            .first()
        )
        return (last or 0) + 1

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def add_question(
        *, section: BriefingSection,
        text: str, instructions: str = '',
        question_type: str = BriefingQuestion.QUESTION_TYPE_TEXT,
        allowed_response_formats=None,
        options=None,
        is_required: bool = True,
        reference_document_ids=None, actor=None,
    ) -> BriefingQuestion:
        if not section.template.is_editable():
            raise BriefingTemplateNotDraftError(
                'Só templates em rascunho podem ser editados.'
            )
        text = (text or '').strip()
        if not text:
            raise BriefingError('O enunciado da pergunta é obrigatório.')

        question = BriefingQuestion.objects.create(
            section=section,
            text=text,
            instructions=(instructions or '').strip(),
            question_type=question_type,
            allowed_response_formats=(
                list(allowed_response_formats)
                if allowed_response_formats is not None
                else list(BriefingQuestion.DEFAULT_ALLOWED_FORMATS)
            ),
            options=list(options) if options else [],
            position=BriefingTemplateService._next_question_position(section),
            is_required=bool(is_required),
        )

        if reference_document_ids:
            BriefingTemplateService._attach_reference_documents(
                question=question,
                document_ids=reference_document_ids,
                workspace=section.template.workspace,
            )

        BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
            template=section.template,
            kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_QUESTION_CREATED,
            user=actor,
            section_id=section.id,
            question_id=question.id,
            content={'text': question.text},
        )
        return question

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def update_question(
        *, question: BriefingQuestion,
        text: str = None, instructions: str = None,
        question_type: str = None,
        allowed_response_formats=None,
        options=None,
        is_required: bool = None,
        reference_document_ids=None, actor=None,
    ) -> BriefingQuestion:
        if not question.section.template.is_editable():
            raise BriefingTemplateNotDraftError(
                'Só templates em rascunho podem ser editados.'
            )

        fields = []
        if text is not None:
            text = text.strip()
            if not text:
                raise BriefingError('O enunciado não pode ser vazio.')
            question.text = text
            fields.append('text')
        if instructions is not None:
            question.instructions = instructions.strip()
            fields.append('instructions')
        if question_type is not None:
            question.question_type = question_type
            fields.append('question_type')
        if allowed_response_formats is not None:
            question.allowed_response_formats = list(allowed_response_formats)
            fields.append('allowed_response_formats')
        if options is not None:
            question.options = list(options)
            fields.append('options')
        if is_required is not None:
            question.is_required = bool(is_required)
            fields.append('is_required')

        if fields:
            question.save(update_fields=fields + ['updated_at'])

        if reference_document_ids is not None:
            question.reference_documents.clear()
            BriefingTemplateService._attach_reference_documents(
                question=question,
                document_ids=reference_document_ids,
                workspace=question.section.template.workspace,
            )

        BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
            template=question.section.template,
            kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_QUESTION_UPDATED,
            user=actor,
            section_id=question.section_id,
            question_id=question.id,
            content={'fields': fields or ['references']},
        )
        return question

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def delete_question(
        *, question: BriefingQuestion, actor=None,
    ) -> None:
        if not question.section.template.is_editable():
            raise BriefingTemplateNotDraftError(
                'Só templates em rascunho podem ser editados.'
            )
        template = question.section.template
        section_id = question.section_id
        question_id = question.id
        question.delete()
        BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
            template=template,
            kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_QUESTION_DELETED,
            user=actor,
            section_id=section_id,
            question_id=question_id,
        )

    @staticmethod
    @transaction.atomic
    def reorder_questions(
        *, section: BriefingSection, ordered_ids: list, actor=None,
    ) -> None:
        if not section.template.is_editable():
            raise BriefingTemplateNotDraftError(
                'Só templates em rascunho podem ser editados.'
            )
        questions = {
            str(q.id): q
            for q in BriefingQuestion.objects.filter(section=section)
        }
        for qid in ordered_ids:
            if str(qid) not in questions:
                raise BriefingQuestionNotFoundError(
                    f'Pergunta {qid} não pertence à sessão.'
                )

        for i, qid in enumerate(ordered_ids):
            q = questions[str(qid)]
            q.position = _REORDER_OFFSET + i
            q.save(update_fields=['position'])

        for i, qid in enumerate(ordered_ids):
            q = questions[str(qid)]
            q.position = i + 1
            q.save(update_fields=['position'])

        BriefingTemplateService._log_event(
            template=section.template,
            kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_QUESTION_REORDERED,
            user=actor,
            section_id=section.id,
            content={'ordered_ids': [str(x) for x in ordered_ids]},
        )

    # ==================================================================
    # HELPERS
    # ==================================================================

    @staticmethod
    def _attach_reference_documents(
        *, question: BriefingQuestion, document_ids, workspace,
    ) -> None:
        from apps.documents.models import Document
        ids = [str(d) for d in document_ids if d]
        if not ids:
            return
        docs = list(
            Document.objects.filter(id__in=ids, workspace=workspace)
        )
        if docs:
            question.reference_documents.set(docs)

================================================================================
ARQUIVO 56 DE 75
