        )

        events = briefings_public.get_recent_events(
            briefing_a.id, workspace_a,
        )
        assert len(events) >= 1
        assert any(e['kind'] == 'note.created' for e in events)

    def test_other_workspace_returns_empty(
        self, workspace_b, briefing_a,
    ):
        events = briefings_public.get_recent_events(
            briefing_a.id, workspace_b,
        )

"""
Testes de prazo e reabertura.

O modelo `Briefing` tem 4 estados: in_progress / paused / completed
/ cancelled. Estados que existiam antes (`closed`, `expired`) foram
removidos e fundidos em `cancelled` — controlado por `cancelled_at`
e `expires_at`. Os testes abaixo refletem o contrato atual.
"""

from datetime import timedelta

import pytest
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.briefings.models import Briefing, BriefingEvent
from apps.briefings.services import BriefingService
from apps.briefings.exceptions import BriefingReopenError


@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestReopen:

    def test_reopen_cancelled_with_expired_deadline_requires_new(
        self, briefing_a, user_owner,
    ):
        # Cancela com prazo expirado
        briefing_a.status = Briefing.STATUS_CANCELLED
        briefing_a.cancelled_at = timezone.now() - timedelta(days=1)
        briefing_a.expires_at = timezone.now() - timedelta(days=1)
        briefing_a.save(update_fields=[
            'status', 'cancelled_at', 'expires_at',
        ])

        with pytest.raises(BriefingReopenError):
            BriefingService.reopen(
                briefing=briefing_a, actor=user_owner,
            )

    def test_reopen_cancelled_with_past_new_deadline_fails(
        self, briefing_a, user_owner,
    ):
        briefing_a.status = Briefing.STATUS_CANCELLED
        briefing_a.cancelled_at = timezone.now() - timedelta(days=1)
        briefing_a.expires_at = timezone.now() - timedelta(days=1)
        briefing_a.save(update_fields=[
            'status', 'cancelled_at', 'expires_at',
        ])

        past = timezone.now() - timedelta(hours=1)
        with pytest.raises(BriefingReopenError):
            BriefingService.reopen(
                briefing=briefing_a, actor=user_owner,
                new_expires_at=past,
            )

    def test_reopen_cancelled_with_future_deadline_succeeds(
        self, briefing_a, user_owner,
    ):
        briefing_a.status = Briefing.STATUS_CANCELLED
        briefing_a.cancelled_at = timezone.now() - timedelta(days=1)
        briefing_a.expires_at = timezone.now() - timedelta(days=1)
        briefing_a.save(update_fields=[
            'status', 'cancelled_at', 'expires_at',
        ])

        future = timezone.now() + timedelta(days=7)
        b = BriefingService.reopen(
            briefing=briefing_a, actor=user_owner,
            new_expires_at=future,
            reason='Prorrogado por pedido do cliente.',
        )

        assert b.status == Briefing.STATUS_IN_PROGRESS
        assert b.expires_at is not None
        assert b.expires_at >= future - timedelta(seconds=2)

        ev = BriefingEvent.objects.filter(
            briefing=b, kind=BriefingEvent.KIND_REOPENED,
        ).order_by('-created_at').first()
        assert ev is not None
        assert ev.content.get('reason') == 'Prorrogado por pedido do cliente.'

    def test_reopen_cancelled_without_deadline_succeeds(
        self, briefing_a, user_owner,
    ):
        # Cancela SEM prazo expirado (não precisa de novo prazo)
        BriefingService.cancel(briefing=briefing_a, actor=user_owner)
        briefing_a.refresh_from_db()
        assert briefing_a.status == Briefing.STATUS_CANCELLED
        assert briefing_a.has_expired() is False

        b = BriefingService.reopen(
            briefing=briefing_a, actor=user_owner,
            reason='Reaberto manualmente.',
        )
        assert b.status == Briefing.STATUS_IN_PROGRESS

    def test_reopen_paused_succeeds(self, briefing_a, user_owner):
        BriefingService.pause(briefing=briefing_a, actor=user_owner)
        briefing_a.refresh_from_db()
        assert briefing_a.status == Briefing.STATUS_PAUSED

        b = BriefingService.reopen(
            briefing=briefing_a, actor=user_owner,
        )
        assert b.status == Briefing.STATUS_IN_PROGRESS

    def test_reopen_completed_without_deadline_succeeds(
        self, briefing_a, user_owner,
    ):
        briefing_a.status = Briefing.STATUS_COMPLETED
        briefing_a.completed_at = timezone.now()
        briefing_a.save(update_fields=['status', 'completed_at'])

        b = BriefingService.reopen(
            briefing=briefing_a, actor=user_owner,
        )
        assert b.status == Briefing.STATUS_IN_PROGRESS
        assert b.completed_at is None

    def test_reopen_in_progress_fails(self, briefing_a, user_owner):
        # Já está in_progress — não é estado reabrível
        with pytest.raises(BriefingReopenError):
            BriefingService.reopen(
                briefing=briefing_a, actor=user_owner,
            )

"""
Testes do BriefingSendService — envio manual pelo arquiteto.

Cobrem:
    - Envio com sucesso registra evento `manual_send`
    - Envio sem e-mail cadastrado levanta erro
    - Reenvio cria um novo evento
    - Isolamento por workspace (não é possível enviar
      briefing de outro workspace)
"""

import pytest
from unittest.mock import patch

from apps.briefings.models import BriefingEvent
from apps.briefings.services import BriefingSendService
from apps.briefings.exceptions import BriefingError
from apps.accounts.services.email import EmailService


@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestSendService:

    def test_send_to_client_success(
        self, briefing_a, user_owner, client_a,
    ):
        with patch.object(
            EmailService, 'send_briefing_to_client', return_value=1,
        ) as mock_send:
            result = BriefingSendService.send_to_client(
                briefing=briefing_a,
                actor=user_owner,
                to_email=None,  # usa cliente.email
                message='Segue em anexo.',
            )

        assert result['sent'] is True
        assert result['to'] == client_a.email
        assert 'event_id' in result

        mock_send.assert_called_once()
        call_kwargs = mock_send.call_args.kwargs
        assert call_kwargs['to_email'] == client_a.email
        assert call_kwargs['message'] == 'Segue em anexo.'

        event = BriefingEvent.objects.get(id=result['event_id'])
        assert event.kind == 'manual_send'
        assert event.user_id == user_owner.id
        assert event.content['to'] == client_a.email

    def test_send_to_alternate_email(
        self, briefing_a, user_owner,
    ):
        alt = 'outro@endereco.com'
        with patch.object(
            EmailService, 'send_briefing_to_client', return_value=1,
        ):
            result = BriefingSendService.send_to_client(
                briefing=briefing_a,
                actor=user_owner,
                to_email=alt,
            )

        assert result['to'] == alt
        event = BriefingEvent.objects.get(id=result['event_id'])
        assert event.content['to'] == alt

    def test_send_without_email_raises(
        self, briefing_a, user_owner, client_a,
    ):
        client_a.email = ''
        client_a.save(update_fields=['email'])

        with pytest.raises(BriefingError):
            BriefingSendService.send_to_client(
                briefing=briefing_a,
                actor=user_owner,
                to_email=None,
            )

    def test_resend_creates_new_event(
        self, briefing_a, user_owner,
    ):
        with patch.object(
            EmailService, 'send_briefing_to_client', return_value=1,
        ):
            r1 = BriefingSendService.send_to_client(
                briefing=briefing_a,
                actor=user_owner,
            )
            r2 = BriefingSendService.send_to_client(
                briefing=briefing_a,
                actor=user_owner,
            )

        assert r1['event_id'] != r2['event_id']

        events = BriefingEvent.objects.filter(
            briefing=briefing_a,
            kind='manual_send',
        )
        assert events.count() == 2

    def test_send_on_completed_briefing_works(
        self, briefing_a, user_owner, portal_session_a,
    ):
        """Reenvio funciona mesmo após conclusão."""
        # Conclui
        from apps.briefings.services import BriefingService
        questions = briefing_a.snapshot_questions()
        for q in questions:
            BriefingService.submit_answer(
                briefing_id=briefing_a.id,
                portal_session=portal_session_a,
                question_id=q['id'],
                content_text='ok',
            )

        briefing_a.refresh_from_db()
        assert briefing_a.status == 'completed'

        with patch.object(
            EmailService, 'send_briefing_to_client', return_value=1,
        ):
            result = BriefingSendService.send_to_client(
                briefing=briefing_a,
                actor=user_owner,
            )

        assert result['sent'] is True

"""
Testes do fluxo sequencial.

Cobrem o requisito "só responde a próxima pergunta elegível".
"""

import pytest

from apps.briefings.models import (
    Briefing,
    BriefingAnswer,
    BriefingEvent,
)
from apps.briefings.services import BriefingService
from apps.briefings.exceptions import InvalidQuestionSequenceError


@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestSequence:

    def test_answer_first_question(
        self, briefing_a, portal_session_a,
    ):
        questions = briefing_a.snapshot_questions()
        first = questions[0]

        answer = BriefingService.submit_answer(
            briefing_id=briefing_a.id,
            portal_session=portal_session_a,
            question_id=first['id'],
            content_text='Minha rotina é tranquila.',
        )

        assert answer.state == BriefingAnswer.STATE_ANSWERED
        assert answer.latest_content == 'Minha rotina é tranquila.'

    def test_cannot_answer_question_out_of_order(
        self, briefing_a, portal_session_a,
    ):
        questions = briefing_a.snapshot_questions()
        third = questions[2]  # nunca respondida, e não é a próxima

        with pytest.raises(InvalidQuestionSequenceError):
            BriefingService.submit_answer(
                briefing_id=briefing_a.id,
                portal_session=portal_session_a,
                question_id=third['id'],
                content_text='Pulando fila.',
            )

    def test_flow_completes_when_all_answered(
        self, briefing_a, portal_session_a,
    ):
        questions = briefing_a.snapshot_questions()

        for q in questions:
            BriefingService.submit_answer(
                briefing_id=briefing_a.id,
                portal_session=portal_session_a,
                question_id=q['id'],
                content_text=f'Resposta para {q["id"]}',
            )

        briefing_a.refresh_from_db()
        assert briefing_a.status == Briefing.STATUS_COMPLETED
        assert briefing_a.completed_at is not None

    def test_review_requested_remains_next_eligible(
        self, briefing_a, portal_session_a,
    ):
        questions = briefing_a.snapshot_questions()
        first = questions[0]

        # Responde
        BriefingService.submit_answer(
            briefing_id=briefing_a.id,
            portal_session=portal_session_a,
            question_id=first['id'],
            content_text='Minha rotina...',
        )

        # Cliente pede revisão na mesma pergunta
        BriefingService.request_review(
            briefing=briefing_a,
            portal_session=portal_session_a,
            question_id=first['id'],
            message='Quero complementar.',
        )

        # Próxima elegível é de novo a pergunta 1
        nxt = BriefingService.get_next_eligible_question(briefing_a)
        assert str(nxt['id']) == str(first['id'])

    def test_answer_event_created_per_submission(
        self, briefing_a, portal_session_a,
    ):
        """
        Reenvio de resposta exige `request_review` antes.

        O fluxo é:
            1. Cliente responde.
            2. Cliente pede revisão (volta a ser elegível).
            3. Cliente responde de novo.
            4. Dois eventos `answered` ficam registrados no histórico.
        """
        questions = briefing_a.snapshot_questions()
        first = questions[0]

        # 1ª resposta
        BriefingService.submit_answer(
            briefing_id=briefing_a.id,
            portal_session=portal_session_a,
            question_id=first['id'],
            content_text='Primeira resposta.',
        )

        # Solicita revisão — reabre a pergunta
        BriefingService.request_review(
            briefing=briefing_a,
            portal_session=portal_session_a,
            question_id=first['id'],
            message='Quero reformular.',
        )

        # 2ª resposta — agora permitida, pois a pergunta voltou
        # a ser a próxima elegível
        BriefingService.submit_answer(
            briefing_id=briefing_a.id,
            portal_session=portal_session_a,
            question_id=first['id'],
            content_text='Segunda resposta (após review).',
        )

        answered_events = BriefingEvent.objects.filter(
            briefing=briefing_a,
            kind=BriefingEvent.KIND_ANSWERED,
            question_id=first['id'],
        )
        assert answered_events.count() == 2

    def test_content_invalid_format_rejected(
        self, briefing_a, portal_session_a,
    ):
        from apps.briefings.exceptions import InvalidResponseFormatError

        questions = briefing_a.snapshot_questions()
        third = questions[2]  # allowed_response_formats=['text']

        # Responder as duas primeiras para liberar a terceira
        first, second = questions[0], questions[1]
        for q in (first, second):
            BriefingService.submit_answer(
                briefing_id=briefing_a.id,
                portal_session=portal_session_a,
                question_id=q['id'],
                content_text='ok',
            )

        # Agora `third` é a próxima elegível. Ela só aceita texto;
        # enviamos nada para forçar erro de formato.
        with pytest.raises(InvalidResponseFormatError):
            BriefingService.submit_answer(
                briefing_id=briefing_a.id,
                portal_session=portal_session_a,
                question_id=third['id'],
                content_text='',
            )

"""
Testes do histórico estrutural (BriefingTemplateEvent).

NOTA: os testes de `set_active` foram removidos porque o conceito
de `is_active` foi extinto — o estado do template agora é único e
vive em `status` (draft / published / archived).
"""

import pytest

from apps.briefings.models import (
    BriefingTemplate,
    BriefingTemplateEvent,
)
from apps.briefings.services import BriefingTemplateService


@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestTemplateEvents:

    def test_create_template_emits_event(self, workspace_a, user_owner):
        tpl = BriefingTemplateService.create_template(
            workspace=workspace_a,
            name='Novo Template',
            actor=user_owner,
        )
        events = BriefingTemplateEvent.objects.filter(template=tpl)
        assert events.count() == 1
        assert events.first().kind == 'template_created'

    def test_add_section_emits_event(
        self, draft_template, user_owner,
    ):
        BriefingTemplateService.add_section(
            template=draft_template,
            title='Sessão 1',
            actor=user_owner,
        )
        assert BriefingTemplateEvent.objects.filter(
            template=draft_template, kind='section_created',
        ).count() == 1

    def test_add_question_emits_event(
        self, draft_template, user_owner,
    ):
        section = BriefingTemplateService.add_section(
            template=draft_template, title='S', actor=user_owner,
        )
        BriefingTemplateService.add_question(
            section=section, text='Pergunta?', actor=user_owner,
        )
        assert BriefingTemplateEvent.objects.filter(
            template=draft_template, kind='question_created',
        ).count() == 1

    def test_delete_question_emits_event(
        self, draft_template, user_owner,
    ):
        from apps.briefings.models import BriefingQuestion
        section = BriefingTemplateService.add_section(
            template=draft_template, title='S', actor=user_owner,
        )
        q = BriefingTemplateService.add_question(
            section=section, text='Pergunta?', actor=user_owner,
        )
        BriefingTemplateService.delete_question(
            question=q, actor=user_owner,
        )
        assert BriefingTemplateEvent.objects.filter(
            template=draft_template, kind='question_deleted',
        ).count() == 1

    def test_publish_emits_event(self, draft_template, user_owner):
        BriefingTemplateService.add_section(
            template=draft_template, title='S', actor=user_owner,
        )
        BriefingTemplateService.publish_template(
            template=draft_template, actor=user_owner,
        )
        assert BriefingTemplateEvent.objects.filter(
            template=draft_template, kind='template_published',
        ).count() == 1

"""
Testes da API de versões de template.

Cobrem o contrato do Bloco de Versionamento + carregamento sob demanda:

    1. GET /versions/ retorna metadados SEM `structure`
    2. GET /versions/{id}/ retorna o detalhe COM `structure`
    3. Autorização: view vs manage
    4. Isolamento por workspace
    5. Versão inexistente → 404
    6. Excluir versão publicada → 400/409 (bloqueado)
    7. Excluir versão draft/archived → 204
"""

import pytest

from apps.briefings.models import BriefingTemplateVersion
from apps.briefings.services import BriefingTemplateVersionService


URL_LIST = '/api/briefing-templates/{tid}/versions/'
URL_DETAIL = '/api/briefing-templates/{tid}/versions/{vid}/'


# =========================================================================
# HELPERS
# =========================================================================

def _ensure_published_version(template, actor):
    """
    Garante que o template tem uma versão publicada.
    Retorna a versão.
    """
    current = BriefingTemplateVersionService.get_current_published(template)
    if current is not None:
        return current
    draft = BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
        template=template, actor=actor,
    )
    BriefingTemplateVersionService.publish(version=draft, actor=actor)
    return draft


# =========================================================================
# LISTAGEM — metadados apenas
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestVersionsList:

    def test_list_returns_metadata_without_structure(
        self, owner_client, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        _ensure_published_version(published_template, user_owner)

        url = URL_LIST.format(tid=published_template.id)
        resp = owner_client.get(url)
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        assert isinstance(resp.data, list)
        assert len(resp.data) >= 1

        first = resp.data[0]
        # Chaves esperadas no list
        assert 'id' in first
        assert 'version_number' in first
        assert 'status' in first
        assert 'sections_count' in first
        assert 'questions_count' in first
        assert 'published_at' in first
        assert 'created_at' in first
        # CRÍTICO: não deve conter structure
        assert 'structure' not in first

    def test_list_only_own_workspace(
        self, other_client, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        _ensure_published_version(published_template, user_owner)
        url = URL_LIST.format(tid=published_template.id)
        resp = other_client.get(url)
        # Template não pertence ao workspace → 404
        assert resp.status_code == 404

    def test_list_unauthorized_user_gets_403_or_400(
        self, outsider_client, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        _ensure_published_version(published_template, user_owner)
        url = URL_LIST.format(tid=published_template.id)
        resp = outsider_client.get(url)
        assert resp.status_code in (400, 403)


# =========================================================================
# DETALHE — com structure
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestVersionDetail:

    def test_detail_returns_full_structure(
        self, owner_client, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        version = _ensure_published_version(published_template, user_owner)

        url = URL_DETAIL.format(
            tid=published_template.id, vid=version.id,
        )
        resp = owner_client.get(url)
        assert resp.status_code == 200

        # Detalhe inclui structure completa
        assert 'structure' in resp.data
        structure = resp.data['structure']
        assert isinstance(structure, dict)
        assert 'sections' in structure
        assert len(structure['sections']) == 2  # fixture tem 2 sessões

        # Cada sessão tem questions
        for sec in structure['sections']:
            assert 'questions' in sec

    def test_detail_404_for_unknown_version(
        self, owner_client, published_template,
    ):
        import uuid
        url = URL_DETAIL.format(
            tid=published_template.id, vid=uuid.uuid4(),
        )
        resp = owner_client.get(url)
        assert resp.status_code == 404

    def test_detail_404_for_other_workspace(
        self, other_client, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        version = _ensure_published_version(published_template, user_owner)
        url = URL_DETAIL.format(
            tid=published_template.id, vid=version.id,
        )
        resp = other_client.get(url)
        assert resp.status_code == 404

    def test_detail_unauthorized_user_gets_403_or_400(
        self, outsider_client, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        version = _ensure_published_version(published_template, user_owner)
        url = URL_DETAIL.format(
            tid=published_template.id, vid=version.id,
        )
        resp = outsider_client.get(url)
        assert resp.status_code in (400, 403)


# =========================================================================
# DELETE
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestVersionDelete:

    def test_cannot_delete_published_version(
        self, owner_client, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        version = _ensure_published_version(published_template, user_owner)
        assert version.status == BriefingTemplateVersion.STATUS_PUBLISHED

        url = URL_DETAIL.format(
            tid=published_template.id, vid=version.id,
        )
        resp = owner_client.delete(url)
        # Bloqueado pelo service (400)
        assert resp.status_code in (400, 409)

        # A versão continua existindo
        assert BriefingTemplateVersion.objects.filter(id=version.id).exists()

    def test_can_delete_draft_version(
        self, owner_client, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        # Cria uma draft extra
        draft = BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
            template=published_template, actor=user_owner,
        )
        assert draft.status == BriefingTemplateVersion.STATUS_DRAFT

        url = URL_DETAIL.format(
            tid=published_template.id, vid=draft.id,
        )
        resp = owner_client.delete(url)
        assert resp.status_code == 204

        assert not BriefingTemplateVersion.objects.filter(id=draft.id).exists()

    def test_delete_404_for_other_workspace(
        self, other_client, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        draft = BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
            template=published_template, actor=user_owner,
        )
        url = URL_DETAIL.format(
            tid=published_template.id, vid=draft.id,
        )
        resp = other_client.delete(url)
        assert resp.status_code == 404

        # Não foi excluída
        assert BriefingTemplateVersion.objects.filter(id=draft.id).exists()


# =========================================================================
# ISOLAMENTO DE CACHE
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestVersionSemantics:
    """
    Garantias de semântica que o frontend depende:

    - draft pode ser alterada
    - published é imutável (após publicação, sua estrutura não muda)
    """

    def test_published_version_does_not_change_after_new_draft(
        self, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        version_v1 = _ensure_published_version(published_template, user_owner)
        v1_structure = dict(version_v1.structure)

        # Cria uma nova versão draft — não deve alterar v1
        BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
            template=published_template, actor=user_owner,
        )

        version_v1.refresh_from_db()
        assert version_v1.structure == v1_structure
        assert version_v1.status == BriefingTemplateVersion.STATUS_PUBLISHED

"""
Testes de versionamento (Bloco 2 — P1).

Cobrem:
    - Criar rascunho clonando versão publicada
    - Criar rascunho em branco
    - Publicar versão arquiva a anterior
    - Nova versão NÃO afeta execuções antigas
    - Diff identifica mudanças reais
    - Diff respeita isolamento por workspace

RESTAURAÇÃO (restore_into_template):
    - Substitui a estrutura do modelo pela da versão escolhida
    - Despublica automaticamente um template `published`
    - Bloqueia se versão é de outro template
    - Aborta se estrutura é inválida (sem alterar nada)
    - Ignora documentos inexistentes/de outro workspace
    - Contabiliza referências ignoradas no evento
    - Cria nova versão draft refletindo o estado
    - Preserva briefings em execução
    - Grava evento de auditoria
"""

import pytest

from apps.briefings.models import (
    Briefing,
    BriefingTemplate,
    BriefingSection,
    BriefingQuestion,
    BriefingTemplateVersion,
    BriefingTemplateEvent,
)
from apps.briefings.services import (
    BriefingTemplateService,
    BriefingTemplateVersionService,
)
from apps.briefings.exceptions import BriefingError


# =========================================================================
# CRIAÇÃO
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestCreateVersion:

    def test_create_draft_clone_current(
        self, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        current = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.get_current_published(
                published_template,
            )
        )
        assert current is not None

        new_version = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
                template=published_template,
                actor=user_owner,
                blank=False,
            )
        )
        assert new_version.status == 'draft'
        assert new_version.version_number == current.version_number + 1
        assert new_version.structure.get('sections')
        assert len(new_version.structure['sections']) == 2

    def test_create_draft_blank(
        self, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        new_version = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
                template=published_template,
                actor=user_owner,
                blank=True,
            )
        )
        assert new_version.status == 'draft'
        assert new_version.structure.get('sections') == []


# =========================================================================
# PUBLICAÇÃO
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestPublishVersion:

    def test_publish_archives_previous(
        self, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        previous = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.get_current_published(
                published_template,
            )
        )

        new_version = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
                template=published_template,
                actor=user_owner,
            )
        )
        BriefingTemplateVersionService.publish(
            version=new_version, actor=user_owner,
        )

        previous.refresh_from_db()
        assert previous.status == 'archived'
        assert new_version.status == 'published'

        current = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.get_current_published(
                published_template,
            )
        )
        assert current.id == new_version.id

    def test_publish_requires_structure(
        self, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        new_version = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
                template=published_template,
                actor=user_owner,
                blank=True,
            )
        )
        with pytest.raises(BriefingError):
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.publish(
                version=new_version, actor=user_owner,
            )


# =========================================================================
# PRESERVAÇÃO
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestVersionPreservesExecutions:

    def test_new_version_does_not_change_existing_execution(
        self, briefing_a, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        old_snapshot = dict(briefing_a.structure_snapshot)
        old_version_id = str(briefing_a.template_version_id)

        new_version = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
                template=published_template,
                actor=user_owner,
            )
        )
        BriefingTemplateVersionService.publish(
            version=new_version, actor=user_owner,
        )

        briefing_a.refresh_from_db()
        assert briefing_a.structure_snapshot == old_snapshot
        assert str(briefing_a.template_version_id) == old_version_id

    def test_new_version_used_by_new_executions(
        self, briefing_a, published_template, client_a,
        workspace_a, user_owner,
    ):
        from apps.briefings.services import BriefingService

        new_version = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
                template=published_template,
                actor=user_owner,
            )
        )
        BriefingTemplateVersionService.publish(
            version=new_version, actor=user_owner,
        )

        new_briefing = BriefingService.create_briefing(
            template=published_template,
            client=client_a,
            workspace=workspace_a,
            actor=user_owner,
        )
        assert new_briefing.template_version_id == new_version.id
        assert briefing_a.template_version_id != new_version.id


# =========================================================================
# DIFF
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestDiff:

    def test_diff_no_changes(
        self, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        data = BriefingTemplateVersionService.diff_against_published(
            published_template,
        )
        assert data['has_baseline'] is True
        assert data['summary']['total_changes'] == 0

    def test_diff_detects_added_question(
        self, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        section = published_template.sections.first()
        BriefingQuestion.objects.create(
            section=section,
            text='Nova pergunta adicionada',
            position=99,
            is_required=True,
            allowed_response_formats=['text'],
        )

        data = BriefingTemplateVersionService.diff_against_published(
            published_template,
        )
        assert data['summary']['questions_added'] == 1
        added = data['questions']['added'][0]
        assert added['text'] == 'Nova pergunta adicionada'

    def test_diff_detects_changed_question(
        self, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        q = published_template.sections.first().questions.first()
        q.text = 'Texto completamente diferente'
        q.save(update_fields=['text'])

        data = BriefingTemplateVersionService.diff_against_published(
            published_template,
        )
        assert data['summary']['questions_changed'] >= 1
        changed_ids = {c['id'] for c in data['questions']['changed']}
        assert str(q.id) in changed_ids

    def test_diff_no_baseline(self, draft_template, user_owner):
        data = BriefingTemplateVersionService.diff_against_published(
            draft_template,
        )
        assert data['has_baseline'] is False
        assert data['base_version'] is None


# =========================================================================
# RESTAURAÇÃO — substitui estrutura do modelo
# =========================================================================

def _make_draft_with_two_sections(template, workspace, actor):
    """
    Zera a estrutura atual e recria 2 seções com 1 pergunta cada.
    Retorna a nova versão em rascunho gerada.
    """
    BriefingSection.objects.filter(template=template).delete()

    s1 = BriefingSection.objects.create(
        template=template, title='Alpha', position=1,
    )
    BriefingQuestion.objects.create(
        section=s1, text='Pergunta A1', position=1,
        is_required=True, allowed_response_formats=['text'],
    )
    BriefingQuestion.objects.create(
        section=s1, text='Pergunta A2', position=2,
        is_required=False, allowed_response_formats=['text'],
    )

    s2 = BriefingSection.objects.create(
        template=template, title='Beta', position=2,
    )
    BriefingQuestion.objects.create(
        section=s2, text='Pergunta B1', position=1,
        is_required=True, allowed_response_formats=['text'],
    )

    return BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
        template=template, actor=actor,
    )


@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestRestoreInto:

    def test_restore_replaces_structure(
        self, draft_template, user_owner,
    ):
        # Prepara uma versão com estrutura conhecida
        source = _make_draft_with_two_sections(
            draft_template, draft_template.workspace, user_owner,
        )

        # Agora mexe na estrutura atual para ficar diferente
        BriefingSection.objects.filter(template=draft_template).delete()
        s_extra = BriefingSection.objects.create(
            template=draft_template, title='Extra', position=1,
        )
        BriefingQuestion.objects.create(
            section=s_extra, text='Pergunta Extra 1', position=1,
            is_required=True, allowed_response_formats=['text'],
        )
        assert draft_template.sections.count() == 1

        # Restaura a v anterior
        new_version = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.restore_into_template(
                template=draft_template,
                source_version=source,
                actor=user_owner,
            )
        )

        # 1) Nova versão em draft refletindo a estrutura restaurada
        assert new_version.status == 'draft'
        assert new_version.template_id == draft_template.id
        assert len(new_version.structure['sections']) == 2

        # 2) Tabelas do template refletem a estrutura restaurada
        draft_template.refresh_from_db()
        sections = list(
            draft_template.sections
            .order_by('position')
            .prefetch_related('questions')
        )
        assert len(sections) == 2
        assert sections[0].title == 'Alpha'
        assert sections[1].title == 'Beta'
        assert sections[0].questions.count() == 2
        assert sections[1].questions.count() == 1

    def test_restore_auto_unpublishes_when_published(
        self, published_template, user_owner,
    ):
        """
        Contrato atual: `restore_into_template` DESPUBLICA
        automaticamente um template `published` antes de substituir
        a estrutura. Este é o comportamento aprovado no modal do
        frontend. O teste valida que o template termina em `draft`
        e a estrutura é substituída.
        """
        current = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.get_current_published(
                published_template,
            )
        )
        assert current is not None

        new_version = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.restore_into_template(
                template=published_template,
                source_version=current,
                actor=user_owner,
            )
        )

        # Template foi despublicado automaticamente.
        published_template.refresh_from_db()
        assert published_template.status == 'draft'

        # Nova versão em rascunho foi criada.
        assert new_version.status == 'draft'
        assert new_version.template_id == published_template.id

    def test_restore_wrong_template(
        self, draft_template, workspace_b, user_owner, user_other,
    ):
        # Cria um template "estranho" no outro workspace
        other_template = BriefingTemplate.objects.create(
            workspace=workspace_b,
            name='Outro',
            status=BriefingTemplate.STATUS_DRAFT,
            created_by=user_other,
        )
        other_version = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
                template=other_template, actor=user_other, blank=True,
            )
        )

        with pytest.raises(BriefingError):
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.restore_into_template(
                template=draft_template,
                source_version=other_version,
                actor=user_owner,
            )

    def test_restore_invalid_structure_aborts(
        self, draft_template, user_owner,
    ):
        # Cria uma versão "válida" primeiro
        version = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
                template=draft_template, actor=user_owner,
            )
        )
        # Envenena a estrutura — pergunta sem texto
        version.structure = {
            'snapshot_version': 1,
            'template_name': draft_template.name,
            'sections': [
                {
                    'id': 's1',
                    'title': 'Quebrada',
                    'description': '',
                    'position': 1,
                    'questions': [
                        {'id': 'q1', 'text': '', 'question_type': 'text'},
                    ],
                },
            ],
        }
        version.save(update_fields=['structure'])

        # Estado antes
        sections_before = list(
            draft_template.sections.values_list('id', flat=True)
        )

        with pytest.raises(BriefingError):
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.restore_into_template(
                template=draft_template,
                source_version=version,
                actor=user_owner,
            )

        # Nada mudou
        sections_after = list(
            draft_template.sections.values_list('id', flat=True)
        )
        assert sections_before == sections_after

    def test_restore_ignores_missing_documents(
        self, draft_template, user_owner, workspace_a,
    ):
        """
        Referência a document inexistente não bloqueia.
        É ignorada e contabilizada.
        """
        import uuid

        bogus_doc_id = str(uuid.uuid4())

        version = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.create_draft_from_template(
                template=draft_template, actor=user_owner,
            )
        )
        version.structure = {
            'snapshot_version': 1,
            'template_name': draft_template.name,
            'sections': [
                {
                    'id': 's1',
                    'title': 'Com ref quebrada',
                    'description': '',
                    'position': 1,
                    'questions': [
                        {
                            'id': 'q1',
                            'text': 'Pergunta com ref',
                            'question_type': 'text',
                            'allowed_response_formats': ['text'],
                            'is_required': True,
                            'reference_document_ids': [bogus_doc_id],
                        },
                    ],
                },
            ],
        }
        version.save(update_fields=['structure'])

        new_version = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.restore_into_template(
                template=draft_template,
                source_version=version,
                actor=user_owner,
            )
        )
        assert new_version.status == 'draft'

        # A pergunta existe, sem referências
        q = BriefingQuestion.objects.get(
            section__template=draft_template,
            text='Pergunta com ref',
        )
        assert q.reference_documents.count() == 0

        # Evento de auditoria registra a referência ignorada
        event = (
            BriefingTemplateEvent.objects
            .filter(
                template=draft_template,
                kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_TEMPLATE_UPDATED,
                content__action='restore_from_version',
            )
            .order_by('-created_at')
            .first()
        )
        assert event is not None
        assert event.content['ignored_references_count'] == 1

    def test_restore_does_not_touch_existing_briefings(
        self, published_template, user_owner, workspace_a, client_a,
    ):
        """
        Briefings criados a partir do modelo continuam com o snapshot
        original, mesmo depois de restaurarmos.
        """
        from apps.briefings.services import BriefingService

        # Cria um briefing a partir do published_template
        briefing = BriefingService.create_briefing(
            template=published_template,
            client=client_a,
            workspace=workspace_a,
            actor=user_owner,
        )
        snapshot_before = dict(briefing.structure_snapshot)

        # Agora despublique e restaure para uma estrutura diferente
        BriefingTemplateService.unpublish_template(
            template=published_template, actor=user_owner,
        )
        published_template.refresh_from_db()

        current = (
            BriefingTemplateVersionService.get_current_published(
                published_template,
            )
        )
        assert current is not None

        BriefingTemplateVersionService.restore_into_template(
            template=published_template,
            source_version=current,
            actor=user_owner,
        )

        briefing.refresh_from_db()
        assert briefing.structure_snapshot == snapshot_before

    def test_restore_logs_event(
        self, draft_template, user_owner,
    ):
        source = _make_draft_with_two_sections(
            draft_template, draft_template.workspace, user_owner,
        )

        # Limpa
        BriefingSection.objects.filter(template=draft_template).delete()

        BriefingTemplateVersionService.restore_into_template(
            template=draft_template,
            source_version=source,
            actor=user_owner,
        )

        event = (
            BriefingTemplateEvent.objects
            .filter(
                template=draft_template,
                kind=BriefingTemplateEvent.KIND_TEMPLATE_UPDATED,
                content__action='restore_from_version',
            )
            .order_by('-created_at')
            .first()
        )
        assert event is not None
        assert event.user_id == user_owner.id
        assert (
            event.content['source_version_id'] == str(source.id)
        )
        assert event.content['sections_created'] == 2
        assert event.content['questions_created'] == 3

ARQUIVO 75 DE 75

"""
Views HTML do módulo briefings.

    /fluxo/                                         → painel do arquiteto (Mesa)
    /w/<public_id>/briefings/<briefing_id>/         → detalhe autenticado
    /portal/briefings/<briefing_id>/respostas/      → dados brutos (cliente)
    /w/<public_id>/briefings/<briefing_id>/resumo/  → dados brutos (arquiteto)

v22:
    - FluxoView passa `workspace_public_id` no contexto, para que o
      botão "Editar briefing" da Mesa consiga montar a URL correta.
"""

from django.http import Http404
from django.shortcuts import redirect, render
from django.views import View

from apps.workspaces.helpers import resolve_workspace_or_redirect
from apps.workspaces.models import WorkspaceMembership
from apps.workspaces.queries import WorkspaceQueries
from apps.briefings.models import Briefing
from apps.briefings.api.serializers import PortalBriefingSerializer
from apps.briefings.queries import BriefingQueries


# =========================================================================
# HELPERS LOCAIS
# =========================================================================

def _resolve_current_workspace(request):
    """
    Retorna o Workspace ativo a partir de `request.session['current_workspace_id']`
    ou `None` se não houver sessão válida.

    Não redireciona. O chamador decide o que fazer.
    """
    workspace_id = request.session.get('current_workspace_id')
    if not workspace_id:
        return None
    return WorkspaceQueries.get_for_user(workspace_id, request.user)


def _workspace_public_id(workspace):
    """
    Resolve o identificador público do workspace usado nas URLs
    (`/w/<public_id>/...`).

    Tenta, nesta ordem:
        public_id → slug → id

    Isso permite funcionar mesmo se o modelo Workspace expuser o
    identificador sob outro nome.
    """
    if workspace is None:
        return ''
    return str(
        getattr(workspace, 'public_id', '')
        or getattr(workspace, 'slug', '')
        or workspace.id
    )


# =========================================================================
# MESA — PAINEL DO ARQUITETO
# =========================================================================

class FluxoView(View):
    """
    Renderiza a página da Mesa (painel principal do arquiteto).

    Contexto:
        workspace_public_id → identificador público do workspace ativo,
                              consumido por `mesa.js` para montar as URLs
                              de navegação ("Editar briefing" etc.).

    Se o usuário não estiver logado → redireciona para /login/.
    Se não houver workspace ativo na sessão → renderiza mesmo assim,
    com `workspace_public_id` vazio. O frontend exibe a lista vazia
    e evita ações que dependem do workspace.
    """

    def get(self, request):
        if not request.user.is_authenticated:
            return redirect('/login/')

        workspace = _resolve_current_workspace(request)
        public_id = _workspace_public_id(workspace)

        return render(request, 'fluxo.html', {
            'workspace_public_id': public_id,
        })


# =========================================================================
# DETALHE AUTENTICADO DO BRIEFING (PÁGINA HTML)
# =========================================================================

class BriefingDetailView(View):
    """
    Página autenticada do briefing para o arquiteto.

    Resolve o workspace pelo `public_id` da URL e valida que o
    usuário é membro. Se o briefing não existir naquele workspace,
    volta para a Mesa.
    """

    def get(self, request, public_id, briefing_id):
        if not request.user.is_authenticated:
            return redirect('/login/')

        workspace, error = resolve_workspace_or_redirect(request, public_id)
        if error is not None:
            return error

        is_member = WorkspaceMembership.objects.filter(
            workspace=workspace, user=request.user,
        ).exists()
        if not is_member:
            return redirect('/workspaces/entrar/')

        briefing = Briefing.objects.filter(
            id=briefing_id, workspace=workspace,
        ).first()
        if briefing is None:
            return redirect('/fluxo/')

        return render(request, 'briefing_detail.html', {
            'workspace': workspace,
            'briefing_id': str(briefing.id),
        })


# =========================================================================
# RESPOSTAS BRUTAS — VISÃO DO CLIENTE
# =========================================================================

class BriefingRawAnswersView(View):
    """
    Página HTML com as RESPOSTAS BRUTAS na visão do CLIENTE.

    - Perguntas + respostas do cliente
    - Imagens escolhidas / enviadas (moodboard pessoal)
    - Sem referências do arquiteto

    Depende do `ClientPortalMiddleware` para injetar
    `request.portal_client`. Sem sessão do portal → volta para /portal/.
    """

    def get(self, request, briefing_id=None):
        client = getattr(request, 'portal_client', None)
        if client is None:
            return redirect('/portal/')

        briefing = BriefingQueries.get_for_client(client, briefing_id)
        if briefing is None:
            raise Http404('Briefing não encontrado.')

        briefing.mark_expired_if_needed()

        payload = PortalBriefingSerializer(
            context={'request': request},
        ).to_representation(briefing)

        return render(
            request,
            'portal/briefing_raw_client.html',
            {'briefing': payload},
        )


# =========================================================================
# RESPOSTAS BRUTAS — VISÃO DO ARQUITETO
# =========================================================================

class BriefingArchitectRawView(View):
    """
    Página HTML com as RESPOSTAS BRUTAS na visão do ARQUITETO.

    - Perguntas + respostas (incluindo transcrições)
    - Referências do arquiteto
    - Imagens escolhidas / enviadas pelo cliente (moodboard)
    - Filtros por sessão e busca
    - Exportar PDF (mode='architect')
    """

    def get(self, request, public_id, briefing_id):
        if not request.user.is_authenticated:
            return redirect('/login/')

        workspace, error = resolve_workspace_or_redirect(request, public_id)
        if error is not None:
            return error

        is_member = WorkspaceMembership.objects.filter(
            workspace=workspace, user=request.user,
        ).exists()
        if not is_member:
            return redirect('/workspaces/entrar/')

        briefing = Briefing.objects.filter(
            id=briefing_id, workspace=workspace,
        ).select_related('client', 'workspace', 'template').first()
        if briefing is None:
            return redirect('/fluxo/')

        payload = PortalBriefingSerializer(
            context={'request': request},
        ).to_representation(briefing)

        return render(
            request,
            'portal/briefing_raw_architect.html',
            {'briefing': payload},
        )

================================================================================
FIM DA LISTAGEM
================================================================================
